Method: DART-S采用三项核心组件：(1) 斜坡面悬架预调节——在起飞前改变俯仰、俯仰率和车轮自旋；(2) 局部标定映射——预测每个悬架动作如何重塑状态-预算对；(3) 支持感知选择器——结合预测偏移、局部结果证据与区间可达性筛选；此外还有精确配对审计报告剩余权限。

Result: 在72个独立BeamNG会话的600次新测试中，所有正、负和边界查询均遵循预设分支。在40°/13 m/s边界处，DART-S实现24/24触地后姿态标准成功，而DART为0/24（会话级Holm校正p=0.0234）。在11.5 m/s速度下，0.35秒时序动作实现23/24成功，静态预设为0/24（p=0.0156）。200 rad/s指令防护在所有600次测试中驱动系统硬限越界为零。

Conclusion: DART-S通过起飞前悬架预调节有效扩展了角动量控制权限预算，显著提升了跳板起飞后姿态恢复的成功率，同时保证了驱动系统的安全边界，证明了状态-预算对的主动重塑是提升空中力矩控制能力的可行途径。

Abstract: Airborne torque reaction cannot recover takeoff errors beyond the wheel angular-momentum budget. DART-S applies ramp-face suspension preconditioning to change pitch, pitch rate, and wheel spin before liftoff, thereby shifting the queried state and altering the remaining authority budget. To predict how each suspension action reshapes this state-budget pair, DART-S employs a local calibration map. A support-aware selector combines the predicted shift with local outcome evidence and an interval-reachability screen; an exact-pair audit reports residual authority. Across 600 new runs in 72 independent BeamNG sessions, every positive, negative, and boundary query follows its prespecified branch. At the confirmed 40°/13 m/s boundary, DART-S attains 24/24 post-touchdown attitude-criterion successes versus 0/24 for DART (session-level Holm-adjusted p=0.0234). At 11.5 m/s, a 0.35 s timing action attains 23/24 versus 0/24 for the static preset (p=0.0156). The 200 rad/s command guard keeps drivetrain hard-limit exceedance at zero across all 600 runs. The source code will be available at https://github.com/MeridianCAS/DART-S

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Incident-Data Robustness Analysis of the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2026): How a Community-Expert Ranking Holds Up Against a Large-Scale LLM Incident Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19266)
*Kyriakos "Rock" Lambros,Steve Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含7,714个快照、6,639个标注条目的LLM安全事件语料库（来自CVE、GHSA、OSV和AIAAIC），使用贝叶斯测量误差模型校正分类器精度/召回率后得出基于事件的排名，并与OWASP Top 10专家排名进行对比。结果显示两者一致性很弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%区间跨越零），但专家排名依然稳健；四个前沿分类器在预注册测试中均未胜过基线（平衡准确率0.863），地面真值检验确认基线排序稳健（Spearman ρ=0.918）。这是工作组成员探索性分析，非官方OWASP发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications是安全从业者社区基于专家共识给出的风险排名，但该排名是否与真实事件数据一致尚不清楚。作者提出更窄的问题：对照真实事件记录，专家排名是否与数据相符？这旨在以实证数据检验专家直觉排名的有效性。

Method: 作者从CVE、GHSA、OSV和AIAAIC四个来源收集大规模LLM安全事件语料库（7,714个快照，6,639个按20项分类法标注），采用贝叶斯测量误差模型校正每个类别计数中的分类器精度和召回率偏差，得出基于事件的排名。2026候选列表以固定权重（专家投票0.75、数据0.25）融合两个信号。通过Cohen's κ评估排名一致性，并通过预注册的四个前沿分类器bake-off和地面真值检验（Spearman ρ）评估稳健性。

Result: 专家排名与事件数据排名的一致性很弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%置信区间跨越零），但专家排名依然稳健。四个前沿分类器在预注册bake-off中均未胜过事件发生率基线的平衡准确率0.863；地面真值检验确认基线排序保持有效（Spearman ρ=0.918 vs 保留的真值）。

Conclusion: 专家共识与真实事件数据之间存在显著分歧，但专家排名本身具有稳健性。数据可用于在有限权重下（0.25）修正而非推翻专家共识。研究明确声明这是两位工作组成员的探索性分析，不取代官方OWASP排名或流程。

Abstract: The OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications ranks the risks that a community of security practitioners judges most important. We ask a narrower question: checked against the record of real incidents, does that expert ranking agree with the data? We assembled a large-scale corpus of LLM-security incidents (7,714 snapshotted and 6,639 labeled against the 20-entry taxonomy) drawn from CVE, GHSA, OSV, and AIAAIC, and derived an incident-based ranking with a Bayesian measurement-error model that corrects each category's count for classifier precision and recall. The 2026 candidate list blends the two signals at fixed weights, 0.75 on the expert vote and 0.25 on the data, so the corpus corrects the consensus without overturning it. The agreement between the two rankings is weak: Cohen's $κ\approx 0.20$, with a 90% interval that crosses zero. The expert ranking is nonetheless robust. A pre-registered bake-off of four frontier classifiers returns no winner. None beats the incidence floor's balanced accuracy of 0.863. A ground-truth check leaves the floor's ordering (Spearman $ρ= 0.918$ against held-out truth) in place. This is an exploratory analysis by two working-group members, not the official OWASP release, and it does not supersede the official list or process.

</details>


### [206] [ABEAT: Efficient and Anonymous Encryption for ABE-based Dynamic Group Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19302)
*Hongmiao Yu,Silas Richelson,Jiachen Chen,K. K. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABEAT，一个高效且匿名的动态群组通信系统，在基于属性的加密（ABE）基础上引入图命名空间，通过新的匿名KP-ABE方法隐藏密文中的明文属性以维护接收者匿名性。ABEAT的接收者验证速度快，非目标接收者的解密开销比现有最优方案（如HVE）降低超过90倍，甚至比不提供匿名的FABEO还低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态群体间的机密通信需要灵活高效的多对多通信能力，且在许多应用场景（如应急响应）中，维护消息接收者的匿名性至关重要。现有的基于ABE的群组通信方案在处理接收者匿名性方面存在不足，且非目标接收者的解密开销过高。

Method: 利用属性基加密（ABE）实现机密群组通信，并结合基于图的命名空间构建高效框架，支持群组的动态形成和变更。提出新的匿名KP-ABE方法，在密文中隐藏明文属性以维护接收者匿名性，同时防御多种试图破坏匿名性的攻击。

Result: ABEAT提供快速的接收者验证，使得非目标接收者的解密开销比现有最优方案（如隐藏向量加密HVE）降低超过90倍，甚至比不提供匿名性的FABEO方案还低40%。

Conclusion: ABEAT在保证接收者匿名性的同时实现了显著优于现有方案的高效性，为非目标接收者大幅降低了解密开销，为匿名动态群组通信提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential communication among a dynamic group of participants that ensures flexible and efficient many-to- many communication is highly desired capability. We leverage attribute-based encryption (ABE) for confidential group communication and enhance it by a graph-based namespace to create an efficient framework that allows groups to be formed and changed dynamically. In this paper, we focus on the important additional need to maintain the anonymity of recipients of a message, when using ABE for group communication for a variety of usage scenarios (e.g., emergency response).
  We propose ABEAT, an efficient and anonymous dynamic group communication system that also minimizes overhead on receivers who are not the intended recipients of a message. In ABEAT, we propose a new anonymous KP-ABE approach to maintain recipient anonymity. ABEAT hides the clear attribute in the ciphertext of KP-ABE, and also prevents several attacks that seek to break anonymity. ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>


### [207] [Aray: Deterministic-First Synthesis of Benign Artifacts for YARA Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19387)
*Emanuel C. A. Valente,Lourenço A. P. Júnior,Leonardo Gonçalves Chahud,Júlio Cezar Estrella,Marcus Botacin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Aray，一个确定性优先的YARA解释器和正向测试样本（fixture）合成器。它通过构造性归一化、字符串与整数证词提取、碰撞检查布局及ELF/PE/通用序列化，在不使用模型生成后端源码的前提下，为YARA规则合成不保留恶意行为的正向匹配样本。在416条公共规则上实现97.6%的整体构建成功率，所有可构建样本均与原始规则匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YARA规则易于分发，但用于演示正向匹配的恶意软件样本难以分发，给存储、持续集成、灾难恢复演练和可复现扫描器验证带来困难。仅嵌入字面量不足以构造替代样本，因为YARA条件可组合备选项、计数、偏移、整型读取和可执行容器约束，且生成文件不应复现恶意行为。

Method: Aray采用确定性优先方法：模型可提出构造性归一化或类型化提取回退方案，但绝不能提供后端源码或二进制结构。常规代码负责验证归一化规则、推导字符串与整型证词、执行提取、路由、碰撞检查布局以及ELF、PE或通用序列化；仅有残余的归一化语义交由有界模型判断器处理。

Result: 在416条公共规则条目上，归一化阶段无需模型协助接受182条，经模型归一化后接受234条。构造性预检通过406条，所有被接受的fixture均匹配原始上游规则。整体成功率406/416（97.6%），可构造规则中406/406，另有10个预期的预检排除，零扫描器不匹配或构造失败。不可达端点确认实现阶段模型调用为零。

Conclusion: Aray证明了在不依赖于模型生成源码的前提下，能够高置信度地为YARA规则合成正向验证样本。原始规则oracle机制确保了fixture与源规则的一致性；但证明对全部可能文件成立是更强且独立的目标。两个锚定正则失败已在最终运行前修复，因此结果属于修复后系统状态，而非留出集估计。

Abstract: A YARA rule is easy to distribute, but the malware sample used to demonstrate a positive match is not. This complicates storage, continuous integration, disaster-recovery exercises, and reproducible scanner validation. Constructing a replacement fixture requires more than embedding literals: YARA conditions can combine alternatives, counts, offsets, integer reads, and executable-container constraints, while the resulting file should not reproduce malware behavior. Positive validation is existential: it requires one file-level member of a rule's match set, not reconstruction of the originating sample. We present Aray, a deterministic-first YARA interpreter and positive-fixture synthesizer. Models may propose constructive normalizations or typed extraction fallbacks, but never backend source or binary structure. Conventional code validates normalized rules, derives string and integer witnesses, and performs extraction, routing, collision-checked layout, and ELF, PE, or generic serialization. Only residual normalization semantics reach a bounded model judge. We evaluated Aray over 416 public-rule entries. Normalization accepted 182 entries without model assistance and 234 after model normalization. Constructibility preflight admitted 406 entries, and every admitted fixture matched its upstream original rule. This yields 406/416 (97.6%) overall and 406/406 among constructible rules, with ten expected preflight dispositions and no scanner mismatches or construction failures. An unreachable endpoint confirmed zero model invocations during realization. The original-rule oracle validates generated fixtures against their source rules; proving implication for all possible files is a separate, stronger objective. Two anchored-regex failures were repaired before the final run, so these are post-fix systems results, not a held-out estimate.

</details>


### [208] [Redactable blockchains and polynomial equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19401)
*Alexander Demin,Alexey Ovchinnikov,Vladimir Shpilrain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了新的工具，用于构建具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构（redactable authenticated data structures）。该构造的安全性基于多变量多项式方程求逆的困难性，当前被认为对量子攻击是安全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的发展，传统的基于数论难题（如大整数分解、离散对数）的密码学方案面临被量子算法（如Shor算法）破解的风险。因此需要构建在后量子时代仍然安全的数据结构认证方案。

Method: 提出一种新的单向函数构造，其求逆等价于求解多变量多项式方程组（元数大于1）。由于目前不存在能高效求解多变量多项式方程组的已知量子算法，该单向函数被认为具有后量子安全性，并以此为基础构建可编辑认证数据结构。

Result: 成功开发了构建后量子安全可编辑认证数据结构的新工具，其安全性建立在多变量多项式方程求逆难题之上。

Conclusion: 通过利用多变量多项式方程组的求解困难性作为安全基础，实现了可用于构建可编辑认证数据结构的后量子安全新工具。在合理选择参数的条件下，该方案目前没有已知的高效量子攻击算法。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>


### [209] [A Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Oral Cancer Screening on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19462)
*Lena D. Swamikannan,Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Jay S. Patel,C. S. Mani,Lakshmi Narayana,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实用的联邦学习（FL）框架，支持跨地域分布的AI医疗研究人员协作开发口腔癌筛查模型。该框架使用Tailscale实现安全网络通信，基于Flower框架进行服务器端聚合，并评估了多种轻量级移动端架构。其中基于MobileNetV4-Conv-S的全局模型（GM-V4）在FedAvg聚合下表现最佳，AUC达0.929，准确率达87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域数据获取受监管要求和患者隐私限制，尤其是临床口腔图像难以获取，而联邦学习可通过去中心化数据集协作训练模型，无需集中或共享患者数据，从而在保护隐私的同时实现稳健的医学AI模型开发。

Method: 采用Tailscale实现客户端设备间安全网络连接与实时通信；使用Flower框架进行服务器端聚合（FedAvg）；客户端部署和编排采用手动配置，不使用企业级FL平台；评估MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large和MobileNetV4-Conv-S等轻量级移动端友好架构，用于智能手机端口腔癌筛查应用。

Result: 在FedAvg聚合的全局轻量级模型中，基于MNv4-Conv-S的全局模型（GM-V4）性能最佳，AUC达到0.929，准确率达到87%。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了无需企业级FL平台即可构建实用的联邦学习框架，为医疗AI研究者提供了一种在地理分布式环境中协作开发口腔癌筛查模型的有效方案，同时兼顾数据隐私和移动端部署可行性。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [210] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS（注意力-嵌入梯度隔离盾），一种轻量级防御方案，通过三项反向路径操作同时封堵Transformer梯度中泄露私有令牌信息的三个通道（注意力输出投影梯度低秩子空间、嵌入梯度行范数稀疏性、MLP扩展梯度可恢复子空间），在不改变架构的前提下将令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中梯度反演攻击可从共享梯度中恢复私有训练文本，构成严重隐私威胁。现有防御仅针对单一泄露通道，要么损害模型效用，要么留下其余结构信号。需要一个能同时封堵所有分析性泄露通道的轻量级防御方案。

Method: AEGIS通过三项反向路径操作封堵三个泄露通道：（1）冻结注意力投影参数从构造上消除通道1；（2）向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声以破坏通道2的令牌存在性信号；（3）向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块模拟噪声以遮蔽通道3。被掩蔽的梯度同时用于本地优化器更新和服务器导出，确保任何一侧都不保留干净信号。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上的评估表明，AEGIS将令牌恢复率降至接近零，对分析型和基于优化的梯度反演攻击均有效，同时保持或提升模型效用。对通道1和通道2提供了形式化安全保障，并针对完全了解机制的适应性强对抗者进行了经验验证。

Conclusion: AEGIS是首个统一封堵Transformer梯度中全部三个信息泄露通道的轻量级防御方法，在保护联邦学习隐私的同时不损害模型效用，为实用化联邦学习隐私保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [211] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双重混淆方法，通过同时混淆更新信息和训练图像来增强联邦学习对图像重建攻击的鲁棒性。该方法结合了基于随机二进制权重的梯度稀疏化技术与图像加密技术，在Vision Transformer图像分类任务上验证，能减少Attention Privacy Leakage（APRIL）攻击恢复的视觉信息，且不引入额外的分类性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能保持数据本地化，但已有研究表明攻击者可从共享的模型更新中重建训练数据，亟需更有效的防护机制。

Method: 提出双重混淆方法：（1）基于随机二进制权重的鲁棒性增强技术，将部分梯度元素随机置零，减少攻击者可获得的原始梯度信息量；（2）图像加密技术，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。两者互补发挥作用。图像加密支持每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥，无需显式密钥共享。

Result: 在Vision Transformer图像分类任务上，该方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且除图像加密本身带来的影响外，未造成额外的分类性能下降。

Conclusion: 该组合方法虽不提供绝对安全性保证，但实验结果证明了梯度修改与图像加密相结合在隐私增强联邦学习中的潜在价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [212] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT 是一个基于信息瓶颈节点概念的APT攻击自适应调查框架，通过图蒸馏、自适应时序图学习和跨时空攻击指纹对齐三个组件，在DARPA E3数据集上达到88.9%-95.7%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS减少约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击具有隐蔽、多阶段的特性，传统检测方法难以有效应对。虽然溯源图能提供丰富的行为上下文，但攻击相关证据往往稀疏且淹没在大量常规系统活动中，导致全图学习计算开销大且难以在长攻击序列中建立关联。

Method: TGL-APT包含三个互补组件：(1) 信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏，抑制溯源冗余同时约束结构失真并保留因果可达性；(2) 自适应时序图学习，随节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3) 跨时空攻击指纹对齐，在不同实体和时间窗口中关联碎片化的可疑活动。最后通过因果扩展和阶段特征重建完整攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上分别取得95.7%、90.9%和88.9%的F1分数，与KAIROS相比在训练时间、检测延迟和内存使用上分别降低约39%、33%和22%。

Conclusion: TGL-APT有效平衡了检测性能、计算效率和调查能力，验证了信息瓶颈节点这一核心观察在高性能溯源图APT分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [213] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会认证和区块链背书的检索增强生成系统，通过零知识协议和多方安全计算生成可验证的信任分数，并通过跨链哈希承诺确保文档和评分的不可篡改性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖集中式数据库，存在文档来源不明、可能被篡改、无法验证可信度等问题。在医疗、金融、物流、法律等对准确性和时效性要求极高的领域，错误或被篡改的文档可能导致严重后果，因此需要建立可验证、不可篡改的信任机制。

Method: TrustRAG采用委员会+区块链架构：(1) 文档在投入使用前由领域专家委员会通过零知识协议进行认证；(2) 委员会的隐藏评分通过安全多方计算（MPC）聚合成客户端可验证的信任分数；(3) 信任分数和文档数据通过哈希承诺跨链联合维护，防止被静默修改或删除；(4) 所有排序结果可独立重放验证。

Result: 系统实现了文档来源可验证、内容防篡改、信任分数可审计的端到端信任链条，RAG检索排序可被独立重放和检查，解决了集中式数据库缺乏监督的问题。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过结合零知识协议、安全多方计算和区块链技术，为RAG系统提供了一种可验证、防篡改、可审计的信任基础设施，尤其适用于高可靠性要求的专业领域。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [214] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将勒索软件攻击建模为基于搜索的软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，利用遗传算法（GA）在系统活动统计偏差硬约束下优化数据加密过程，实现长时间隐蔽的勒索软件部署，成功规避行为监控器的指纹检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件通常采用大规模加密操作，容易被现代防御系统立即检测到。现代威胁呈现出持久性攻击的特征，攻击者希望攻击行为能在系统中潜伏数小时而非数分钟，因此需要一种能够规避行为监控的新攻击范式。

Method: 将勒索软件执行过程重新定义为SBSE优化问题，使用遗传算法（GA）在系统活动基线统计偏差的硬约束条件下优化数据加密策略，使得加密行为在统计上与正常系统活动保持一致，从而规避行为监控器的指纹识别。

Result: 实验表明，经过进化式算法生成的攻击模式能够在指纹识别技术下成功规避行为监控器的检测，验证了搜索方法在生成逃避性恶意软件方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于搜索的方法为生成逃避性恶意软件提供了强大框架，揭示了自动化软件防御面临的新兴挑战。本研究标志着密码攻击范式的转变，从即时大规模加密转向长期隐蔽的优化驱动攻击。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [215] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MaliciousSkillBench，一个综合性的恶意Agent Skill检测基准，整合了13个公共数据源、7,539个规范化唯一身份和9,740个Skill样本（含7,505个恶意和2,235个良性）。通过评估多种检测方法，揭示了跨源泛化能力不足和良性误报率过高两大核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills为LLM智能体提供了可复用的指令包（可能包含脚本、资源和服务配置），这同时创建了恶意行为分发的直接渠道。然而现有恶意Skill数据集分散于不同来源、格式和技术规范中，存在重复及结构相关的内容问题，直接聚合和评估十分困难。需要一个统一、全面、跨源的基准来衡量和推动恶意Skill检测的发展。

Method: 作者构建了MaliciousSkillBench基准：(1) 整合13个公共来源，其中11个贡献核心恶意工件，将8,414条原始恶意记录归一化为7,539个唯一身份，归入4,588个操作结构家族；(2) 经保守跨标签冲突排除后，基准包含9,740个Skill（7,505恶意/2,235良性）；(3) 对4,983个恶意身份协调了11类攻击分类；(4) 评估了3种学习的文本检测器和3种现成Skill扫描器，包括随机划分、结构无关划分和源无关划分三种评估设置。

Result: 学习型检测器在随机划分下Random Macro-F1达0.882-0.932，但源无关评估仅0.653-0.665；最强word TF-IDF SVM在随机/结构无关/源无关评估中分别得0.932/0.916/0.665，恶意召回率95.6%但源外良性误报率高达62.4%。现成扫描器在降低误报的同时显著牺牲恶意召回率。结果表明可靠的恶意Skill检测需更广泛的跨源基准覆盖，以及同时衡量攻击检测与良性误报的联合评估。

Conclusion: 现有检测方法在跨源场景下泛化能力严重不足，且存在检测性能与误报率之间的尖锐权衡。构建可靠恶意Skill检测需要：更全面的跨源基准覆盖，以及能够联合评估攻击检测能力和良性过度标记问题的评估框架。本基准为这一方向提供了统一的基础设施。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [216] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个面向机密计算环境的POSIX兼容文件系统，利用TEE内的简洁密码学承诺和轻量级可信注册表，结合哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树，为持久存储提供端到端完整性和新鲜性保障，无需修改应用程序。通过扩展ZFS实现为ShieldZFS，在标准基准测试下性能与现有先进文件系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算在TEE内保护应用，但存储仍然脆弱。即使有磁盘加密，恶意的云提供商仍可对磁盘状态进行回滚、重放、分叉或篡改，破坏有状态应用所需的完整性和新鲜性保证。现有方案要么假设存储可信，要么开销过高，要么将完整性逻辑压入应用中。

Method: ShieldFS利用简洁密码学承诺表示允许的文件系统状态，承诺保存在TEE内部并在轻量级可信注册表中复制。磁盘数据结构（包括写前日志和存储池）通过哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树进行认证。采用事务和写时复制（CoW）机制原子性地更新持久化文件系统状态和承诺。读取时验证承诺，从而在I/O栈完全不可信的情况下检测回滚、重放和抵赖攻击。实现上通过扩展ZFS得到ShieldZFS。

Result: 通过标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载评估，ShieldZFS提供了强完整性、新鲜性保证，性能与现有最先进文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS证明了在机密计算威胁模型下，无需应用改动即可为持久存储提供端到端完整性和新鲜性保障是可行的，且开销可控，性能可接受。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [217] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath将可验证凭证的撤销状态检查从验证者侧转移到持有者侧，通过零知识证明让持有者证明凭证未被撤销，而验证者仅获知状态结果而不泄漏任何可关联的元数据。论文首次评估了Verkle树在凭证撤销中的适用性，并与稀疏Merkle树进行对比实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证凭证的撤销状态检查可能泄漏重复标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可被用作稳定句柄来关联多次独立的凭证展示，从而破坏持有者的隐私。因此需要一种既能验证撤销状态又不泄漏可关联信息的方案。

Method: ShadowPath将凭证状态查找移至持有者侧：每次展示时，持有者以零知识方式证明凭证在验证者选定的注册表根下未被撤销。论文使用Groth16证明系统，对比了稀疏Merkle树与Verkle树两种数据结构在凭证撤销场景中的表现，并引入会话随机性确保验证者可见的状态数据无法关联两次展示。

Result: 在30次桌面实验中，稀疏Merkle树的Groth16证明中位耗时371.6ms，Verkle树为2.11s；验证分别耗时3.70ms和7.55ms。两部主流移动设备上Groth16 Verkle证明耗时约3s。结果表明更短的身份验证路径并不必然带来更廉价的零知识证明。

Conclusion: ShadowPath实现了撤销状态检查的隐私保护转移。在会话值独立性的假设下，验证者可见的状态数据无法揭示两次展示是否使用同一凭证，该保证排除发行者-验证者共谋及同步流量场景。Verkle树虽缩短路径深度，但KZG认证的高成本使其在零知识证明中并不占优。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [218] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指令微调LLM的标准化工分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（含边界情况）。在受控网络安全测试床上评估了三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B、GPT-OSS 20B），与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch统计异常检测对比。Meta Llama 3.1 8B表现最佳，准确率89.3%、F1分数91.8%，边界场景检测率80%，大幅优于Wazuh（准确率52.0%）和OpenSearch（准确率49.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有日志异常检测方法存在明显局限：基于规则的检测（如Wazuh）依赖预定义规则，难以捕捉未知异常；统计方法（如OpenSearch）缺乏语义理解能力；LLM方法虽能理解语义上下文，但受提示词构建、噪声日志数据和缺乏端侧特定认证行为数据集等因素影响。因此需要开发能处理边界情况且适应端点特定环境的标准化LLM检测框架。

Method: 1) 构建标准化指令式LLM分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（含边界情况）；2) 搭建受控网络安全测试床，生成端侧特定认证数据，构建包含正常、边界和异常行为场景的精选数据集；3) 采用统一ground-truth严重性框架评估三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B）与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch异常检测的性能对比。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct实现最佳端到端检测性能：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、漏报率11.8%；边界异常场景检测率80%。对比组Wazuh准确率仅52.0%、漏报率68.6%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、漏报率74.5%。Qwen检测性能较低但推理延迟最低且结构化响应有效性100%；GPT-OSS在有效响应下表现出强分类性能。

Conclusion: 基于指令微调LLM的检测框架在日志异常检测中显著优于传统规则检测和统计方法，尤其在边界情况检测上具有突出优势。Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct被确认为最优模型，同时可根据实际需求在延迟（Qwen）和分类精度（GPT-OSS）之间进行权衡选择。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [219] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了人类对现代语音合成工具（2019、2022、2024年发布）生成深度伪造音频的检测能力，发现随着合成技术升级，人类检测性能大幅下降：完全伪造语音的F1分数从约90%降至48%，部分伪造（单句篡改）的严格准确率仅9%，且人类与自动检测器失败方式互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究报道人类对深度伪造语音的检测准确率为70-80%，但这些研究主要基于较旧的语音合成器。随着语音合成技术的快速进步，需要重新评估人类在当前技术水平下的检测能力，以了解真实威胁状况。

Method: 选取2019年（RTVC）、2022年（YourTTS）和2024年（ElevenLabs）发布的三种代表性语音合成工具，招募82名IT专业人员作为被试进行人类检测实验，同时将同一批材料输入六个预训练自动检测器进行基准对比。实验涵盖完全伪造（full spoofs）和部分伪造（partial spoofing，仅篡改一句话）两种条件。

Result: 完全伪造条件下，人类F1分数从RTVC和YourTTS的约90%骤降至ElevenLabs的48%；部分伪造条件下严格准确率仅9%，且77%的情况下被试将伪造句误判为真实语音。人类与自动检测器的失败模式互补，二者均无法可靠定位短时篡改。此外，被试越来越倾向于将真实语音误标为伪造，侵蚀了对未篡改音频的信任。

Conclusion: 人类感知在现代和部分伪造条件下不可靠，亟需程序化验证、来源追溯、水印及段级检测等技术手段来补充人为主观判断。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [220] [COPA: Continual Preference Optimization for Adaptive Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19982)
*Roshan Sood,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: COPA提出了一种持续偏好优化框架，将提示注入防御视为终身学习问题，通过GRPO优化和边际加权经验回放持续适应新型攻击，同时抵御灾难性遗忘，平均降低攻击成功率最高达6.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM提示注入防御主要依赖静态对齐或攻击特定过滤机制，需要随新攻击策略不断重新设计。虽然近期有终身对齐方法处理用户偏好变化，但未考虑能够持续演化以利用先前防御弱点的自适应攻击者，这在真实部署场景中尤为关键。

Method: COPA通过两个核心机制实现持续防御：(1) 基于GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）的增量优化，从新观察到的攻击中持续吸收反馈；(2) 边际加权经验回放，保留先前攻击类别的防御能力以缓解灾难性遗忘，同时保持模型通用能力。

Result: 在终身提示注入攻击流实验中，COPA相比最先进防御方法平均降低攻击成功率4.4倍，最高可达6.3倍。

Conclusion: 持续偏好优化是防御LLM自适应攻击者的有效范式，验证了将防御建模为终身学习问题的可行性，为对抗持续演化的攻击提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLMs remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, where adversarial instructions embedded in user inputs or external content manipulate model behavior and bypass safeguards. Existing defenses are predominantly static, relying on fixed alignment objectives or attack-specific filtering mechanisms that require redesign as new attack strategies emerge. While recent lifelong alignment methods address shifting user preferences, they do not account for adaptive adversaries that continually evolve to exploit weaknesses in previously learned defenses. This limitation is particularly important in real-world deployments, where evolving attack distributions necessitate continual adaptation without sacrificing robustness to previously encountered threats. We present COPA, a continual preference optimization framework that treats prompt-injection defense as a lifelong learning problem. Instead of one-time alignment, COPA incrementally incorporates feedback from newly observed attacks via GRPO-based optimization and uses margin-weighted experience replay to retain defenses against prior attack classes. This enables continuous adaptation to emerging threats while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and preserving general-purpose model capabilities. Across lifelong prompt injection attack streams, COPA reduces attack success rate by up to 6.3x and 4.4x on average compared to state-of-the-art defenses. These results highlight continual preference optimization as an effective paradigm for defending LLMs against adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [221] [EchoCoT: Extracting Hidden Chain-of-Thought from Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20055)
*Yiting Qu,Ziqing Yang,Chi Cui,Ye Leng,Junjie Chu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了如何通过API交互从黑盒大推理模型（LRM）中近逐字提取隐藏思维链（CoT）的问题，提出了名为EchoCoT的多步攻击方法，利用工具调用之间的推理重放面及API返回的保真度信号进行迭代提取，并设计了LLM驱动的优化框架来自动搜索通用注入轨迹。在三个开源和五个前沿专有LRM上验证，开源模型上最高达到66.4%的近逐字提取成功率，专用模型上也表现出显著提取效果（如从Gemini-2.5中提取33,463个token），确立了隐藏CoT提取作为实际安全风险的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿专有大推理模型（LRMs）的隐藏思维链（CoT）痕迹是极具价值的模型资产，但此前尚不清楚这些隐藏CoT能否通过黑盒API交互被直接近逐字提取。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究从黑盒模型中提取隐藏CoT的可行性，为安全风险评估提供依据。

Method: (1) 识别出此前被忽视的"工具调用之间的推理重放表面"（reasoning replay surface）；(2) 提出EchoCoT，一种多步攻击方法，利用API返回的保真度信号（fidelity signals）迭代提取隐藏CoT；(3) 开发基于LLM的优化框架，自动搜索跨数据集通用的有效注入轨迹（universal injection trajectory）。

Result: 在开源LRM上，EchoCoT最高达到66.4%的近逐字提取成功率（提取痕迹长度在目标10%以内，且至少90%的token与目标CoT精确匹配）；同一注入轨迹可泛化至未见数据集，最高达80%提取成功率；对前沿专有LRM（如Gemini-2.5等五个型号），提取的CoT与提供商报告的长度和可用CoT摘要高度吻合，能提取超过33,000 token的超长CoT（如从32,948-token目标中提取33,463个token）。

Conclusion: 隐藏CoT提取已被证实是一种实际可行的安全风险，现有黑盒模型机制无法有效保护隐藏推理过程，必须加强对隐藏CoT资产的保护措施。

Abstract: Hidden chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, especially those from frontier proprietary large reasoning models (LRMs), are valuable model assets. Yet whether these hidden CoTs can be directly extracted from black-box models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically study whether hidden CoTs can be extracted near-verbatim from black-box LRMs through API interactions. We identify a previously overlooked reasoning replay surface between tool calls and develop EchoCoT, a multi-step attack that iteratively extracts hidden CoTs using API-returned fidelity signals. We further develop an LLM-based optimization framework that automatically searches for an effective universal injection trajectory across various datasets. We evaluate EchoCoT on three open-source and five frontier proprietary LRMs. On open-source LRMs, EchoCoT achieves up to 66.4\% near-verbatim extraction success, with the extracted trace length within 10\% of the target and at least 90\% of tokens exactly matching the target CoT. The same injection trajectory also generalizes to unseen datasets, achieving up to 80\% extraction success under the same criterion. For tested frontier proprietary LRMs, a substantial fraction of extracted CoTs closely align with provider-reported reasoning lengths and available CoT summaries. EchoCoT can also extract very long CoTs: on Gemini-2.5, it extracts 33,463 tokens from a 32,948-token target. These results establish hidden-CoT extraction as a practical security risk and highlight the need to better protect hidden CoT assets.

</details>


### [222] [A Meta-Study on Replication Papers in Usable Security & Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20108)
*Christian Mack,Benjamin Berens,Hanna Algedri,Tobias Hilt,Daniela Reimer,Peter Mayer,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文采用混合研究方法，系统调查了可用安全与隐私（usable security and privacy）领域中复制研究（replication）的现状，分析了13个会议/期刊的征稿启事、24篇相关论文，并通过问卷调查作者动机，提出了四项关键洞察和若干实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用安全与隐私研究是一个年轻且快速扩展的领域，其研究标准（如复制研究的标准）仍在发展中。为了理解该领域复制研究的当前状态，进而推动其研究规范化的进程，作者开展了本研究。

Method: 采用混合方法：(1) 审查了跨越安全、隐私和人机交互领域的13个学术平台的征稿启事（Call for Papers）；(2) 系统搜索2016至2025年间发表在这些平台上的复制用户研究论文，共获得24篇；(3) 使用Olszewski等人（2025）提出的复制分类学对这24篇论文进行分类；(4) 向这些论文的作者发放调查问卷，以了解其进行复制研究的动机。

Result: 分析揭示了四个关键发现：(A) 各平台征稿启事缺乏关于复制研究的明确指南；(B) 阅读复制论文时难以确定相对于原始研究的具体修改之处；(C) 严格的精确复制在样本中不存在，约三分之二的论文对原始工作做了多方面的改动；(D) 影响结果的时间性和情境性变化是复制研究最常被引用的动机之一。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，作者为学术平台、研究人员和同行评审者提供了实用的建议，以加强可用安全与隐私研究领域的复制实践。

Abstract: The field of usable security and privacy research is a young and expanding field, which is still developing standards for its research, e.g. regarding replications. We used a mixed-method approach, in order to get a better understanding of the current state of replications in the field of usable security and privacy: (1) we examine the Call for Papers of 13 venues spanning security, privacy, and human-computer interaction; (2) we conduct a systematic search for papers reporting replicated user studies published across these venues between 2016 and 2025, yielding 24 relevant publications; (3) we categorized these 24 papers employing the replication taxonomy proposed by Olszewski et al. (2025); (4) we distributed a survey to the authors of these papers to understand their motivations for conducting replications. Our analysis reveals four key insights: (A) Calls for Papers would benefit from clearer guidelines for authors and reviewers regarding replication work; (B) determining what modifications were made relative to the original study proves difficult when reading replication papers; (C) strict exact replications do not exist in our sample. Approximately two-thirds of the 24 studies altered multiple aspects of the original work; (D) temporal and contextual changes affecting results emerged as one of the most frequently cited motivations for replication. Based on these findings, we offer practical recommendations for venues, researchers, and peer reviewers to strengthen replication practices in usable security and privacy research.

</details>


### [223] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，用于在密文共享数据上训练和推理CellCnn模型，实现稀有疾病相关细胞亚群的隐私保护检测，在CMV和AML基准数据集上保持接近明文版本的准确率，并优于先前隐私保护基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测稀有疾病相关细胞亚群（如白血病、病毒感染）需要大规模、多样化的患者队列，但单个机构难以独立组建，且单细胞数据高度敏感，受现有隐私法规限制无法跨机构共享。因此需要在保护患者隐私的前提下，实现CellCnn模型的协同训练和推理。

Method: 提出一个安全多方计算（MPC）框架，使CellCnn的训练和推理完全在秘密共享数据上执行，确保参与方和计算服务器均无法观测原始患者数据或中间值。与先前方法不同，本方法保留了ReLU激活函数和偏置项等关键架构组件。

Result: 在巨细胞病毒感染（CMV）和急性髓系白血病（AML）两个基准单细胞数据集上，所实现的方法在准确率上接近明文版本的CellCnn，同时优于先前的隐私保护基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在不暴露原始患者数据的前提下，成功保留了CellCnn的完整架构关键组件（如ReLU和偏置项），实现接近明文精度的隐私保护单细胞分析，为跨机构稀有细胞亚群检测提供了可行的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values. Evaluated on benchmark single-cell datasets for cytomegalovirus infection (CMV) and acute myeloid leukaemia (AML), our implementation preserves accuracy close to its plaintext counterpart while outperforming the prior privacy-preserving baseline. In contrast to earlier privacy-preserving approaches that removed components such as ReLU activations and bias terms, our method retains these key parts of the CellCnn architecture and supports accurate analysis without exposing raw patient data.

</details>


### [224] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于区块链的信任管理机制，用于解决移动边缘缓存中恶意边缘服务器和自私辅助节点的问题。通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任值并安全存储于区块链，结合激励机制和认证机制，在考虑移动性、能耗和计算能力约束下，实现信任、缓存容量和成本效率的联合优化。仿真表明即使在10%数据噪声下也能准确区分诚实与恶意节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络流量持续增长使边缘缓存成为降低延迟的关键技术，但面临三大挑战：边缘服务器可能被恶意攻击（删除或篡改缓存内容）、边缘服务器缓存容量有限、辅助移动节点因自私行为不愿共享缓存资源且也可能存在恶意行为。需要一种能够准确识别可信节点并激励资源共享的信任管理机制。

Method: 提出基于区块链的信任管理机制，核心包括：1) 利用智能合约计算直接信任和间接信任值，有效过滤恶意节点；2) 信任度基于移动节点对服务质量的满意度来衡量；3) 信任数据安全存储在区块链上；4) 引入奖励机制激励缓存资源共享以对抗节点自私性；5) 采用基于区块链的认证机制防止节点冒充攻击；6) 在共识过程中考虑移动节点移动性、能耗和计算能力约束，对信任、缓存容量和成本效率进行联合优化。

Result: 仿真结果显示，所提出的方法即使在数据含有10%噪声的情况下，仍能准确区分诚实与恶意的边缘服务器，验证了该信任管理机制的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该基于区块链的信任管理机制能够有效解决移动边缘缓存中的节点信任问题，通过信任评估、激励和认证的综合设计，兼具准确性、安全性和成本效率，为边缘缓存系统的可信协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [225] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Chameleon是一种基于多对多随机流量变形的鲁棒网站指纹防御方法，通过高类内多样性和低类间差异的候选选择、随机映射共享变形目标，以及基于基数树（radix-trie）的同步机制实现Tor可插拔传输部署，在六种防御和五种攻击的对比评估中显著优于现有方案，尤其能有效抵御防御感知自编码器（DAAE）攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网站指纹防御方法大多存在可学习的网页轨迹映射特征，且对对抗训练鲁棒并不意味着对防御感知自编码器（DAAE）攻击鲁棒。这揭示了当前防御体系在真实威胁模型下的脆弱性，亟需一种能从根本上消除可学习映射并增强攻击者不确定性的新型防御方案。

Method: Chameleon采用多对多随机流量变形策略：1）选择具有高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选；2）将每个网页轨迹随机映射到多个候选目标，并允许不同网页共享变形目标以增强攻击者不确定性；3）引入基于基数树（radix-trie）的同步机制，使可插拔传输（PT）端点通过数据包方向前缀识别一致的变形轨迹；4）采用轨迹变异和归一化前缀匹配来降低开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的封闭与开放世界场景中，Chameleon相较Adaptive Tamaraw将对抗训练攻击准确率降低多达36.74%，同时带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%。在GTT23数据集上的DAAE随机森林攻击中，Chameleon将攻击F1分数限制在35.19%，而Adaptive Tamaraw仅能限制到88.22%。真实世界PT网桥评估中，Chameleon大幅削弱强WF攻击并仅引入16.25%的时间开销。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过多对多随机流量变形和实用的Tor部署机制，有效克服了现有防御在可学习映射特征和DAAE攻击下的弱点，在保持低开销的同时实现了显著的防御性能提升，为Tor环境下的网站指纹防御提供了可靠且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>


### [226] [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572)
*Mubassir Serneabat Sudipto,Shakil Ahmed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VQC-ZTI，一种面向触觉互联网服务的分裂平面变分量子分类器零信任保护框架。通过离路径VQC分析加密流量遥测数据、在路径策略引擎应用确定性动作，实现了异常评分与执行的解耦，在保持控制路径稳定性的同时显著提升风险判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网服务将网络事件直接关联到物理执行，安全决策必须在不扰动控制路径的前提下提升风险判别能力。传统方法与控制路径耦合可能导致控制行为的不确定性，需要在零信任框架下实现安全性与控制稳定性的统一。

Method: VQC-ZTI采用分裂平面架构：离路径的变分量子分类器（VQC）分析加密流量遥测数据，在路径的策略引擎应用缓存化的确定性动作（允许、限制、升级、拒绝）。该设计将异常评分与强制实施解耦，允许检测器灵敏度和策略激进程度独立调优。实现采用PyTorch-PennyLane混合框架，基于CESNET聚合流量进行评估。

Result: 在随机、实体分组和时间保持三种划分下，全混合量子神经网络的平均AUC分别达到0.9981、0.9974和0.9941，相比ExtraTrees将误报率分别降低44.6%、49.6%和67.9%。组件时序分解表明批处理VQC评分保持在异步证据路径而非即时实施路径中。

Conclusion: VQC-ZTI证明量子机器学习范式可以应用于触觉互联网的零信任安全保护，通过架构上的分裂平面设计实现了安全决策与控制路径的工程解耦，在不牺牲控制行为可预测性的前提下获得优异的异常检测性能。

Abstract: Tactile Internet services couple cyber events directly to physical actuation, so security decisions must improve risk discrimination without perturbing the control path. This paper presents VQC-ZTI, a split-plane Variational Quantum Classifier framework for zero-trust protection of Tactile Internet services, in which an off-path VQC analyzes encrypted-flow telemetry while an on-path policy engine applies cached deterministic grant, restrict, step-up, and deny actions. By decoupling anomaly scoring from enforcement, VQC-ZTI preserves predictable control behavior and allows detector sensitivity and policy aggressiveness to be tuned independently. We evaluate the framework on CESNET-derived aggregated traffic using random, entity-group, and temporal holdouts with a hybrid PyTorch-PennyLane implementation. The full-hybrid Quantum Neural Network achieves mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9981, 0.9974, and 0.9941 and reduces the false-positive rate relative to ExtraTrees by 44.6%, 49.6%, and 67.9%, respectively. A representative component-timing decomposition further illustrates that batched VQC scoring remains in the asynchronous evidence path rather than the immediate enforcement path.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从脑电（EEG）信号中解码静默阅读时的词汇信息。作者使用单参与者约49小时、约24万词次的密集采样干电极EEG数据，结合CLIP式对比学习框架，证明开放词汇的词级信息可以从EEG中提取，且解码性能随数据量对数线性增长、尚未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内心独白的非侵入式解码面临根本性数据问题：无法收集与自发内心独白配对的脑活动数据；现有替代范式（线索重复式与回顾报告式生成性内心独白）采集缓慢、时间对齐差、且无法验证被试依从性。因此需要一个可扩展、可验证的替代范式来研究内隐语言的脑解码。

Method: 将静默阅读作为可扩展的替代任务。使用单参与者19通道干电极EEG，393次run约49小时，约24万词呈现。文本以快速序列视觉呈现（RSVP），每次试验随机化排版以解耦词身份与低层视觉形式。采用卷积EEG编码器（可选加因果Transformer），以CLIP式对比目标将短时EEG窗口与大型语言模型的隐藏状态嵌入对齐。解码通过词分组top-10检索评估，与置换基线对比；并进行了电极消融和对照分析（区分词级解码、叙事上下文跟踪及位置嵌入引入的非神经位置先验）。

Result: 解码可靠地高于偶然水平，扩展到中频和稀有词，且随训练数据量对数线性增长、无饱和迹象。去除枕叶和后颞电极使词级增益下降约三分之一，但不影响上下文跟踪。对照分析成功区分了词级解码与叙事上下文跟踪及Transformer位置嵌入带来的位置先验效应。

Conclusion: 研究表明开放词汇的词级信息可以从静默阅读的EEG中恢复，且解码受限于数据量而非达到饱和，为未来内心独白解码研究提供了可扩展的词级解码基准。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [228] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督式机器学习的新型直升机起飞重量估计模型，利用Airbus全球在役机队的大规模数据集训练了一个长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络，并遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准进行了学习保证流程，最终在传统航电计算机上验证了该实现在机载关键功能（如机上告警）中的可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量的准确估计对于飞行安全至关重要，尤其是在机载告警等关键功能中。传统方法可能依赖人工计算或简化模型，精度和实时性有限。航空领域日益严格的适航要求（如EASA机器学习应用概念和Eurocae ED-324）需要一套系统化的学习保证流程来确保基于机器学习的航空产品的安全性和可靠性。

Method: 本文采用监督式机器学习方法，利用Airbus全球在役机队的大规模数据集训练长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络用于直升机起飞重量估计。研究流程包括：提出一套完整的机器学习需求（ML Requirements）、创建机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description），并将LSTM网络实现在传统航电计算机平台上，最后对需求进行验证。

Result: 该LSTM重量估计模型成功实现了在传统航电计算机上的部署，并通过需求验证证实其适用于机载关键功能（如机上报警）等飞行中关键应用场景。

Conclusion: 本文证明了遵循航空领域机器学习保证标准（EASA概念文件和Eurocae ED-324）的系统化开发流程可以有效实现机器学习模型在机载关键功能中的部署，为基于ML的航空安全关键功能认证提供了可行的实践路径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [229] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的三角模糊数距离度量d_TR，将线性重缩放直接集成到距离计算中，解决了异构属性不同尺度下需要预归一化的问题，并证明了其满足度量性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊距离度量大多假设所有值处于同一尺度，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时，需要额外的预归一化步骤，这增加了复杂性和潜在误差。需要一个将归一化直接嵌入距离计算的度量方法。

Method: 提出三角模糊重缩放距离(d_TR)，将线性重缩放(LRE)直接集成到模糊数比较的距离计算过程中，使归一化成为比较环节的内在组成部分。通过权重向量对维度进行优先级排序，并正式证明其满足非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式的度量性质。

Result: 证明了d_TR是有界的、尺度不变的和原点不变的，满足度量的所有公理性质，使其适用于异构模糊数据的多种应用场景。

Conclusion: d_TR为异构模糊数据处理提供了一种无需预归一化的距离度量工具，可应用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等领域。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [230] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将量子-经典混合机器学习应用于基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测，通过角度编码和密集角度编码等量子特征映射结合不同纠缠策略构建基于保真度的量子核，并与经典SVM基线比较。结果表明，在片段组学数据上多个20特征量子核配置优于经典SVM，在甲基化数据上经典SVM表现最佳但部分量子模型仍有竞争力，总体支持量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌筛查虽能降低死亡率，但其效果受限于依从性和管理挑战；cfDNA生物标志物虽可补充筛查手段，但因肺癌异质性及高维非线性分子信号，早期检测仍然困难。因此需要探索能有效捕捉非线性特征的新型机器学习方法。

Method: 采用量子-经典混合机器学习框架：先进行特征选择得到20和40特征子集；将特征通过角度和密集角度特征映射编码到量子希尔伯特空间，并采用多种纠缠策略；使用精确statevector模拟计算基于保真度的量子核；将该核与预计算核SVM及核PCA逻辑回归集成，并与基于原始特征的SVM模型进行对比。

Result: 在重复留出验证中，量子核模型在两个数据集上均取得有竞争力的性能。片段组学上若干20特征配置的AUC优于经典SVM基线；甲基化数据上经典SVM AUC最高，但部分量子模型仍具有竞争力且在某些情况下提高了特异性。特征数从20增至40未带来一致性提升且常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法在cfDNA肺癌检测中展现出潜力，系统评估了编码和纠缠设计对分类性能的影响，但特征数量的增加并不总能改善效果，未来仍需进一步优化量子核配置以实现更优的检测性能。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [231] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态心电数据集和Holtercare-Bench基准，解决MLLM在动态心电图分析中缺乏高质量数据与评测的问题。通过信号-视频-文本三模态对齐构建22,980个QA对，评测模型在时序定位、临床诊断和全局总结上的表现，发现现有MLLM在超长病理序列处理上存在显著性能差距，但微调后大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态语言模型在医学应用中表现优异，但大多只擅长静态图像或短期信号分析。在动态心电图这一关键领域，模型面临复杂时序推理和诊断报告生成的挑战，根源在于缺乏高质量数据集和基准评测。

Method: (i) 构建Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态心电数据集，包含从788份临床Holter记录中衍生的22,980个QA对，提出新颖的信号-视频-文本三模态对齐机制；(ii) 提出Holtercare-Bench多模态基准，从时序定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度评估模型能力。(iii) 对主流MLLM进行零样本评测及微调实验。

Result: 零样本评测显示主流MLLM在处理超长病理序列上存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后产生大幅性能提升，验证了数据集和基准的有效性。

Conclusion: 本工作揭示了当前MLLM在电生理学领域的局限性，为长期医学MLLM研究提供了基础性基准，并公开了项目和代码资源。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [232] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)，一种纵向贝叶斯学习框架，利用纵向弥散张量成像(DTI)连续估计阿尔茨海默病(AD)的疾病严重程度，并推导出疾病连续体评分(DCS)来量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置及其不确定性。在ADNI队列上的实验表明DCP优于代表性疾病进展方法，且DCS能准确表征疾病严重程度、具有良好的临床相关性、保持纵向疾病演变并预测未来疾病转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD本质上是连续的生物学过程，而现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像上的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续进展过程。因此需要一种能够从纵向影像中连续估计疾病严重程度的方法。

Method: DCP采用纵向贝叶斯学习框架，将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜在变量，通过联合整合纵向观测与弱临床监督进行推断，并据此提出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)来量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置及不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上，DCP在疾病进展预测方面一致优于代表性方法；综合验证分析表明DCS能够准确表征疾病严重程度、展现强临床相关性、保持纵向疾病演化轨迹，并能预测未来疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种超越传统诊断标签和临床评分的定量影像学表征，可实现AD进展的连续评估，为AD的纵向疾病跟踪和早期干预提供新的影像学生物标志物。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [233] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据集利用的LLM不确定性量化增强方法：通过构建简单分类器，将现有零样本不确定性分数与相似查询的正确性作为特征来预测LLM回答的正确性，在最小计算开销下持续优于现有UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有UQ方法（如言语化置信度和多代采样）能零样本地量化不确定性，但在实际部署前必须评估其在目标数据集上的表现。作者发现可以充分利用这个数据集来显著提升UQ性能。

Method: 利用部署前的目标数据集，构建简单分类器来预测LLM响应正确性。分类器使用两类特征：(1)现有的不确定性分数；(2)相似查询的正确性信息。该方法在现有UQ分数之上进行学习增强，计算开销极低。

Result: 在利用数据集信息后，该方法的性能一致优于现有的零样本不确定性分数，且仅引入极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过简单利用评估数据集的信息，就能以低成本、简单的方式系统性提升LLM不确定性量化性能，适合现实世界应用。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [234] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积和子集干预等机制可解释性测量统一为y=Ax+w的线性测量形式，并提供从低成本测量起步、逐级扩展测量族的实用流程。在合成HMM、Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI和Qwen-2.5-7B等任务上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性领域存在多种测量方法（如patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积、子集干预），它们在不同访问假设下针对不同目标量，但缺乏统一的数学框架来描述其共享的测量结构，导致方法选择和结果比较缺乏理论指导。

Method: 将各测量统一定义为机制断层扫描：对于选定基和干预族，测量形式为y=Ax+w，其中A描述干预，x为目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基失配。提出实用流程：先用最低成本测量，在预期尺度上测试保留干预，校准简单失配，若存在结构化残差则扩展测量族。在控制任务（两HMM模型）中验证观测器与控制器关系；在多个模型上比较不同测量族的表现。

Result: 两HMM模型中控制误差随观测器误差上升，目标改进可能掩盖干扰态变化；仅前向访问下，稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching用更少干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改进局部归因映射；提升测量和Hessian-向量积能恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互；GPT-2-small IOI中Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是最大的保留预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上有限校准使加和式拒答映射足够，无需成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描提供了一个统一的测量语言和实用流程，强调测量族的选择应数据驱动地取决于基和任务的保留干预误差，而非先验地选择某种测量方法。控制任务为观测器质量提供了严格验证，提示研究者应关注观测误差与下游干预效果之间的定量关系。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [235] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了神经网络鲁棒性验证中基于模板（template）加速技术的有效性，提出了联合稳定神经元（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板子集率的差异，并设计了FastCert技术，自动跨层分布模板并在低收益场景下完全摒弃模板，最终在L0验证任务上实现了平均1.13倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模板的证明共享已在特定场景下被证明能加速不完整验证技术，但模板加速在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 首先进行系统性实验研究模板加速的有效性及其限界，提出联合稳定神经元这一新指标用于解释模板子集率的变化；随后提出FastCert技术，能够自动将模板分配到神经网络各层以提升性能影响，若模板不太可能产生加速则完全放弃使用模板。

Result: 研究表明模板子集率在不同场景下差异很大；FastCert在大量基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上，相对于现有模板复用技术实现了平均1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速技术并非在所有场景下都有效，通过联合稳定神经元指标可以预测其适用性，FastCert通过自动分配和动态摒弃模板策略有效提升了验证性能。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [236] [FleetSieve: Decision-Critical Profiling for SLO-Aware LLM Fleet Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19659)
*Huang Cheng,Scott Zhang,Aubert Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FleetSieve是一种用于大语言模型推理服务集群资源分配的自适应性能测量方法，根据资源耦合的SLO感知决策来选择测量配置，在固定比较中比均匀随机profiling节省6.9%的GPU时间（平均节省5.4%），并避免选择违反SLO的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LLM推理服务集群选择张量并行度和副本数量非常困难，因为性能随TP度数变化不具备单调性，且可行配置会随负载变化。穷举profiling虽然能解决不确定性，但会测量大量对最终资源分配没有影响的配置，造成不必要的GPU资源浪费。

Method: FleetSieve根据测量配置对资源耦合的SLO感知集群决策的预期影响来选择测量。它联合建模容量和尾部延迟，比较保守分配和乐观分配，当剩余决策差距低于指定容差时停止测量。

Result: 在固定的H100测量网格上，针对31B参数开放权重模型，FleetSieve使用22,200 GPU秒达到oracle聚合决策，比均匀随机profiling少6.9%的GPU时间。在200个随机reveal顺序下，平均节省5.4%的GPU时间（95% bootstrap CI: 3.5-7.2%）。Chat任务的固定比较节省达21.5%，而Code任务未使用最少的GPU秒数。联合容量和尾部建模避免了选择46.4秒完成p99违规30秒SLO的配置。在16-GPU分配中，不正确的稀疏profile决策最多损失1.93 requests/s和12.4个百分点的max-min fulfillment。

Conclusion: FleetSieve通过测量决策相关的配置（而非穷举）来有效地减少LLM服务集群资源分配中的测量成本，同时通过联合建模容量和尾部延迟来确保SLO合规。Boundary repeats和BurstGPT测量进一步支持了观察到的负载依赖尾部延迟机制。

Abstract: Choosing tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and replica counts for an LLM serving fleet is difficult because performance is not monotonic in TP and the feasible choice can change with load. Exhaustive profiling resolves this uncertainty, but measures many configurations that do not affect the final resource allocation. We present FleetSieve, which selects measurements according to their expected effect on a resource-coupled, SLO-aware fleet decision. FleetSieve models capacity and tail latency jointly, compares conservative and optimistic allocations, and stops when their remaining decision gap is below a specified tolerance. On a fixed H100 measurement grid for a 31B-parameter open-weight model, FleetSieve reaches the oracle aggregate decision using 22,200 GPU-seconds, 6.9% less than uniform random profiling in the fixed comparison. Across 200 random reveal orders, its mean saving over random profiling is 5.4% (95% bootstrap CI: 3.5-7.2%). The fixed-comparison saving is 21.5% for Chat, while FleetSieve does not use the fewest GPU-seconds for Code. Joint capacity and tail modeling also avoids selecting a configuration whose 46.4-second completion p99 violates a 30-second SLO. In a 16-GPU allocation, an incorrect sparse-profile decision loses up to 1.93 requests/s and 12.4 percentage points of max-min fulfillment. Boundary repeats and BurstGPT measurements support the observed load-dependent tail-latency mechanism.

</details>


### [237] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime，一个多分辨率框架，用于预测网络安全事件披露后的短期异常财务损失，并通过动态对比学习目标缓解稀疏监督问题，同时构建了SECURE真实数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全公告等通过网络传播的冲击事件会突然扰乱金融时间序列并造成大量异常损失，但大多数时间序列预测模型仅关注趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件，加之高影响事件稀疏和背景市场噪声的存在，使事件条件下的冲击影响预测极具挑战。

Method: EventTime是一个多分辨率框架，融合了长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据。它包含一个事件融合模块，将时间表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关近期市场模式；同时引入动态对比学习目标，在训练期间构建事件和时间序列感知的正负样本对，以缓解稀疏监督问题。

Result: 在SECURE数据集（对齐网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化/LLM语义特征的现实数据集）上的实验表明，EventTime在估计事件后财务损失方面持续优于最先进的时序和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其具有更强的事件敏感表示、对不完整元数据更强的鲁棒性以及更可解释的短期市场影响估计。

Conclusion: EventTime通过结合多分辨率时序表征、事件融合和动态对比学习，有效解决了事件条件下冲击影响预测的难题，为网络安全披露后的短期市场损失估计提供了更准确、更稳健且更具可解释性的方案。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>
