We propose MS-WDRO, a multi-source Wasserstein distributionally robust graph learning framework that fuses heterogeneous sources via their weighted Wasserstein barycenter, a geometrically principled nominal distribution, then builds an ambiguity ball around it to hedge residual uncertainty. Minimizing worst-case risk yields a tractable regularized Laplacian estimator solved efficiently via a provably convergent ADMM scheme. We establish non-asymptotic guarantees: a finite-sample concentration bound for the empirical barycenter, a pooling bias lower bound proving naive aggregation is suboptimal, and an out-of-sample excess risk bound decaying at a parametric rate with only logarithmic dependence on source count. To calibrate hyperparameters governing robustness, sparsity, and source fusion, we unroll the solver into a differentiable architecture trained end-to-end, achieving data-adaptive calibration beyond cross-validation while retaining interpretability. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and the multi-site ABIDE~I neuroimaging dataset show MS-WDRO consistently outperforms seven baselines in graph recovery, sample efficiency, and downstream diagnostic utility, with the largest gains in the sample-scarce regime.

</details>


### [271] [Auditing Recorded Predictive Lead Service-Line Classifications Against Physical Verification: A Statewide Study of New York](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19922)
*Muhammad Sarmad Sohail*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究分析了纽约州153个根据美国铅铜规则修订版使用预测模型而非物理检查来判定服务管线材料的地区，发现75个（49%）地区记录单一值（零方差）。其中68个可自洽解释，但7个与其自身巡查记录矛盾（远超抽样误差），包括纽约市五个区、东罗切斯特等。纽约市最大案例中43,215个地址全部被模型标记为"已知其他"材料，铅检出率为零，95%置信上限仅0.0085%，而同州其他地区同类方法记录铅或"未知但可能是铅"的比例达12.21%。模型清除人群建筑年代更晚，但控制年代后记录分类法仍检出4.3-31.9%的铅、物理验证1.5-14.5%，模型在所有年代均为零。六种年代感知估计器预测铅管数量为1,150-1,450根。此外，7,782个地址位于1940年前建筑中，且2025年存档快照显示公共侧判定是从客户侧模型输出复制而来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国铅铜规则修订版允许公用事业公司使用预测模型替代物理检查来判定服务管线材料。纽约州公开了每个地址使用的方法，但几乎不存在同一地址既有模型分类又有物理验证的情况，因此需要在公用事业公司内部的人口层面进行验证。本研究旨在筛查所有符合条件的地区，检测是否存在模型系统性低估铅管风险的情况。

Method: 作者筛选了纽约州153个分类了至少100个地址的行政区域，检查其记录是否存在零方差（始终记录同一值）。对75个零方差地区，逐一与其自身物理验证记录比对。对纽约市案例，统计预测模型bucket中的铅记录数并计算95%置信上限，同时将模型分类结果与纽约州其余地区的同类方法进行比较。使用六种年代感知估计器估算预期铅管数量，并检查2025年存档快照确认数据复制来源。

Result: 75个零方差地区中68个可与自身验证或样本量不足解释，但7个与自身巡查团队矛盾。纽约市43,215个模型分类地址全部记录为"已知其他"，铅登记数为零，95%上限为0.0085%，而州内其他地区同方法铅或"未知但可能含铅"比例达12.21%。建筑年代仅解释约三分之一的差异。六种估计器预测铅管数为1,150-1,450。有7,782个模型清除地址位于1940年前建筑，且发现公共侧判定直接复制自客户侧模型输出。

Conclusion: 纽约市等7个地区存在预测模型系统性低估铅服务管线的情况，尤其是纽约市将43,215个地址通过模型全部标记为"已知其他"材料，且存在将客户侧模型输出复制为公共侧判定的证据。即使控制建筑年代因素，模型与其他方法的铅检出率差异仍显著，表明这些零方差记录不能仅因"零方差本身不等于不当行为"而被免责。

Abstract: Under the US Lead and Copper Rule Revisions, a utility may determine a service line's material with a predictive model instead of inspecting it. New York State publishes, per address, which method was used. Almost no address carries both a model classification and a physical verification, so the check is between populations within a utility rather than paired addresses. We screen all 153 New York localities that classified at least 100 addresses this way. Seventy-five (49%), covering 125,990 addresses or 57% of those screened, record one value. Zero variance alone is not misconduct: 68 of the 75 match their own verification or have too little to test. Seven are contradicted by their own crews, six beyond any sampling explanation. Five are boroughs of New York City, which file as one system; one is East Rochester, 550 km away. New York City is the largest case: a predictive model is the recorded basis for 43,215 addresses, and on all of them the recorded material is "Known Other". The city records "Unknown" on 121,779 addresses, 1,880 already excavated, and lead on 120,692. In the model bucket both counts are zero, and the 95% upper bound on the rate is 0.0085%. Across the rest of New York the same method records lead or the hedge "Unknown but could be lead" on 12.21% of 176,888 addresses, a comparison whose weaknesses we report. The model-cleared population is newer, median year built 1984 against 1930, and construction era accounts for about a third of the gap and not the rest: holding era fixed, records-based classification finds lead at 4.3-31.9%, physical verification at 1.5-14.5%, the model in no era. Six era-aware estimators place the expected lead lines among them at 1,150-1,450. Two findings need no comparison: 7,782 of these addresses are in pre-1940 buildings, and the archived 2025 snapshot shows the public-side determination was copied from a customer-side model output.

</details>


### [272] [G-MARK: Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Cooperative Driving via Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19964)
*Bhavya Gupta,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出G-MARK，一个基于认知图谱（KG）的多智能体协作驾驶推理框架，将协作感知中的物体级观测转换为显式溯源感知的知识图谱，保留源归属、可见性、不确定性和冲突信息，以支持自主驾驶中的目标推理、运动预测、控制选择与轨迹预测。相比SOTA基线，遮挡推理准确率提升42.2%，控制选择误差降低13.1%，在轨迹规划精度相当的前提下通信载荷缩小25.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主驾驶系统在部分可观测环境下运行，关键物体可能被遮挡或仅被邻近联网车辆看到。虽然车-车协作可以减少不确定性，但现有协作驾驶方法通常将多智能体证据压缩为潜在特征或隐藏多模态状态，导致无法明确：哪个智能体观测到了哪个物体、物体是否对自车可见，以及冲突证据如何影响下游决策。

Method: G-MARK框架首先将协作的物体级观测转换为显式溯源感知的知识图谱，保留物体假设及其来源归属、自车与协作车可见性、不确定性、冲突、空间关系及规划相关上下文；然后从这些KG中导出共享特征表示，供轻量级任务头用于目标推理、运动预测、控制选择和轨迹预测。

Result: 与SOTA基线相比：遮挡推理准确率提升42.2%，控制选择误差降低13.1%，轨迹规划精度相当，结构化通信载荷缩小25.6倍。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过以显式、可解释的知识图谱形式保留多智能体观测的溯源信息，G-MARK在提升协作感知推理能力的同时显著降低了通信代价，为部分可观测环境下的自主驾驶协作提供了更可靠且更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems must operate under partial observability, where safety-critical objects may be occluded or visible only to neighboring connected vehicles. Vehicle-to-vehicle cooperation can reduce this uncertainty, but existing cooperative driving methods often compress multi-agent evidence into latent features or hidden multimodal states. As a result, they obscure which agent observed each object, whether the object is visible to the ego vehicle, and how conflicting evidence affects downstream decisions. We propose G-MARK, a grounded multi-agent reasoning framework that converts cooperative object-centric observations into explicit provenance-aware knowledge graphs (KGs). The resulting KGs preserve object hypotheses together with their source attribution, ego-versus-partner visibility, uncertainty, conflicts, spatial relations, and planning-relevant context. G-MARK then derives a shared feature representation from these KGs, enabling lightweight task heads to support object reasoning, motion prediction, control selection, and trajectory forecasting. Compared with the state-of-the-art baseline, GMARK improves occlusion reasoning accuracy by 42.2%, reduces control-selection error by 13.1%, and achieves comparable trajectory-planning accuracy with a 25.6x smaller structured communication payload. Our code is available at https://github.com/bhavyagupta98/g-mark.

</details>


### [273] [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994)
*Caterina Doglioni,Akshat Gupta,Thomas Elliott,Hanzila Hussain,Sanjiban Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了曼彻斯特大学两个暑期实习项目的成果，聚焦于基于机器学习的无损数据压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的盈亏平衡点。通过对比ML训练和推理所需基础设施的碳当量与减少磁盘存储所节省的碳当量，讨论了二者的平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习技术在数据压缩领域的应用日益广泛，需要评估这些ML-based压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的实际收益，即其训练和推理产生的碳排放是否能够被存储空间节省带来的碳减排所抵消。

Method: 以ML-based无损压缩算法为例，估算ML训练和推理所需基础设施的碳当量，同时估算因减少磁盘存储需求而节省的碳当量，并对两者进行比较分析以确定盈亏平衡点。

Result: 论文给出了ML压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的碳当量对比估算结果，并确定了碳当量收支平衡点的位置。

Conclusion: 通过两个暑期实习项目，论文提供了一种评估ML-based数据压缩算法环境可持续性的量化框架，明确了训练/推理碳排放与存储碳节省之间的盈亏平衡点。

Abstract: We summarise the outcome of two summer internship projects based at the University of Manchester, focused on the break-even point in terms of environmental sustainability for ML-based data compression algorithms. Using the example of a ML-based lossless compression algorithm, we compare estimates for the carbon-equivalent of the infrastructure needed for ML training and inference with the carbon-equivalent savings from reduced disk storage requirements, and discuss their break-even point.

</details>


### [274] [Scale-Aware Pretraining of Time Series Foundation Models via Multi-Patch Token Alignment and Hybrid Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20005)
*Taihua Chen,Xiang Ma,Yixin Zhang,Tailin Zhan,Manyu Sun,Lizhen Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SATS是一种面向时间序列基础模型的尺度感知token对齐机制，通过将patch size视作显式尺度概念，利用对比学习启发的对齐正则化器统一跨尺度表示空间，并结合随机与连续掩码的混合策略捕获多尺度时间结构。在LSTF基准上MSE提升9.2%、GIFT-Eval MASE提升8.3%，同时模型效率提升65.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在异构数据集上预训练时需处理不同采样频率，现有方法要么使用数据集特定的patch size和独立FFN导致表示碎片化，要么采用固定patch size而忽略固有时间变化，缺乏统一的跨尺度表示方案。

Method: SATS包含三个核心组件：(1) 尺度感知token对齐机制，将patch size视为显式的尺度概念；(2) 对比学习启发的对齐正则化器，用于对齐跨尺度表示空间同时保留不同尺度的独特建模能力；(3) 结合随机掩码与连续掩码的混合掩码策略，以捕获多尺度时间结构。

Result: 在LSTF基准上，SATS相比竞争基线在MSE上提升9.2%，在GIFT-Eval MASE上提升8.3%；持续达到SOTA性能，同时相较先进基线模型效率提升65.6%。

Conclusion: SATS有效解决了时间序列预训练中跨尺度表示的碎片化与固定patch size局限的问题，在性能和效率上均优于现有基线，展示了其在高异质性时间序列预训练中的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Pretraining time series foundation models across heterogeneous datasets necessitates effective handling of varying sampling frequencies. Current methods either employ dataset-specific patch sizes and separate FFNs, leading to fragmented representations, or enforce a fixed patch size that neglects inherent temporal variations. To address this, we propose SATS, featuring a scale-aware token alignment mechanism that treats patch size as an explicit notion of scale. By incorporating a contrastive-inspired alignment regularizer, SATS aligns representation spaces across scales while preserving distinct modeling capacities. Furthermore, a hybrid masking strategy combining random and contiguous masking is introduced to capture multi-scale temporal structures. Experimental results on LSTF benchmarks demonstrate that SATS achieves a 9.2% improvement in MSE and an 8.3% gain in GIFT-Eval MASE compared to competitive baselines. Notably, SATS consistently delivers SOTA performance while achieving a 65.6% increase in model efficiency over advanced baselines, highlighting its effectiveness and scalability in time series pretraining.

</details>


### [275] [Systematic Evaluation of TabPFN-TS for Zero-Shot Probabilistic Heat Load Forecasting in District Heating Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20024)
*Ben Spoek,Karim K. Ben Hicham,Kai Derzsi,Philipp Althaus,Alexander Mitsos,Dirk Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了TabPFN-TS（基于合成数据预训练的时间序列基础模型）在区域供热网络概率热负荷预测中的表现，与Chronos-2等基础模型及传统机器学习基线对比。研究发现每小时24小时预测、12周滚动上下文与气温作为协变量的配置最为高效，TabPFN-TS在确定性精度上接近Chronos-2（CVRMSE 13.06% vs 12.48%），且在经验校准方面更优。最后提出一种多分辨率残差校正预测器以改善长期规划精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统供热负荷预测需要为每个系统单独训练模型，当管网发生变化（新用户接入、改造或运行工况改变）时需反复重训练，成本高昂。零样本时间序列基础模型可通过推理时的上下文学习自适应，无需重复训练，但已有工作主要基于真实数据预训练，存在训练-测试重叠风险。TabPFN-TS采用合成数据预训练，避免了这一问题，但其先验是否能捕获供热动态特征尚属未知，需要系统验证。

Method: 本研究对TabPFN-TS进行系统性评估，包括：(1) 与Chronos-2等时间序列基础模型和传统机器学习基线进行对比；(2) 分析协变量选择、上下文长度、时间分辨率和预测期对各代表性运行周的影响；(3) 对选定配置进行全年验证；(4) 在第二个供热网络上测试可迁移性。基于诊断发现，进一步提出Multi-Resolution Residual-Correction Forecaster，将低频Base Forecaster与短时残差预测器结合以提升长期规划精度。

Result: 每小时24小时预测、12周滚动上下文和气温作为协变量是最佳配置，更长的上下文窗口不提升精度。TabPFN-TS在主数据集上达到CVRMSE 13.06%，与Chronos-2的12.48%接近，在日排名比较中处于临界差异阈值之内。虽然Chronos-2全年聚合误差最低，但TabPFN-TS的经验校准表现更好。

Conclusion: 合成预训练的TabPFN-TS能够有效捕获区域供热动态特征，在无重复训练的条件下达到与传统方法可比甚至更优的预测性能，且具有更好的概率校准能力。12周上下文窗口已足够，无需更长上下文。多分辨率残差校正框架为改善供热预测的长期规划精度提供了新思路。

Abstract: District heating energy hubs require reliable heat load forecasts for efficient operational scheduling. Conventional forecasting workflows train system-specific models on historical data, which can become burdensome when networks change through new consumers, retrofits, or changing operating regimes. Zero-shot time-series foundation models and in-context forecasting offer a promising alternative: they can adapt at inference time from recent observations rather than by repeated retraining. This study systematically evaluates TabPFN-TS against time-series foundation models and trained machine-learning baselines for probabilistic heat load forecasting in district heating networks. Unlike foundation models pretrained on large collections of real time series, TabPFN-TS relies on synthetic pretraining data, which avoids direct pretraining-test overlap but raises the question of whether the learned prior captures district heating dynamics. We analyze covariate choice, context length, temporal resolution, and prediction horizon on representative operating weeks, validate the selected configuration over a full year, and test transferability on a second network. The results identify hourly 24-hour forecasting with a 12-week rolling context and ambient temperature as a parsimonious high-performing configuration; longer context windows do not improve accuracy. TabPFN-TS remains close to Chronos-2 in deterministic accuracy, reaching CVRMSE values of 13.06% versus 12.48% on the main dataset, and lies within the critical-difference threshold in the daily-rank comparison. Although Chronos-2 achieves the lowest aggregate full-year error, TabPFN-TS shows better empirical calibration. Finally, the diagnostic findings motivate a Multi-Resolution Residual-Correction Forecaster that combines a low-frequency Base Forecaster with a short-horizon Residual Forecaster to improve longer-horizon planning accuracy.

</details>


### [276] [CLaST: Context-aware Contrastive VAE for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20025)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Petr Sokerin,Vitaliy Pozdnyakov,Ilya Kuleshov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLaST，一种基于VAE框架的概率多变量时间序列预测模型，通过对比损失函数学习保留观测间上下文相似性的嵌入表示，在九个基准数据集上显著超越现有基线方法，短期预测CRPS提升最高16.4%，长期预测CRPS提升最高48.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率预测方法难以捕捉时间序列内部的时间依赖关系，导致潜在表示的表达能力受限，影响预测精度。

Method: 提出CLaST框架，基于变分自编码器（VAE），核心创新在于引入对比损失函数来学习嵌入表示，使嵌入能够保留观测数据之间的上下文相似性，从而增强潜在空间的表现力。

Result: 在九个广泛采用的基准数据集上进行实验，CLaST一致性地超越强基线方法。短期预测中CRPS最高提升16.4%，NMAE最高提升14.4%；长期预测中CRPS最高提升48.6%，NMAE最高提升25.1%。

Conclusion: CLaST通过对比学习增强VAE的潜在表示能力，在短长期概率时间序列预测任务中均取得了显著的性能优势，验证了上下文相似性保持策略的有效性。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are widely used for time series forecasting in domains such as energy systems, finance, medicine, and transportation. In recent years, deep generative models have shown strong results on probabilistic forecasting, yet many conventional approaches struggle to capture internal temporal dependencies, leading to latent representations with limited expressive power. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{CLaST}, a VAE framework for probabilistic multivariate time series forecasting. Unlike existing generative models, CLaST learns embeddings that preserve contextual similarity between observations through our contrastive loss function. Experiments across nine widely adopted benchmarks demonstrate that CLaST consistently surpasses strong baseline methods. In short-term forecasting tasks, our approach achieves improvements of up to $16.4\%$ in CRPS and $14.4\%$ in NMAE over the second-best method. Furthermore, in long-term prediction CLaST attains superior overall performance, exceeding the second-best method by up to $48.6\%$ and $25.1\%$ in CRPS and NMAE, respectively.

</details>


### [277] [An Inclusive and Lightweight Approach to Federated Continual Learning for Cultural Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20038)
*Ioannis Theologitis,Debin Meng,Stylianos Eleftheriadis,Vasileios Lolis,Konstantinos Votis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCurv-DR，一种轻量级的基于正则化的联邦持续学习策略，用于文化遗产领域的数字化藏品分析。该方法跨客户端和任务累积参数重要性估计以保护已学知识，并仅在固定间隔更新，以最小化通信和计算开销。在WikiArt数据集上的实验表明，该方法能减少灾难性遗忘，并在性能、公平性和能效之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产数据通常分散在不同机构中，受到所有权和访问权限限制，且随时间持续演化。联邦持续学习（FCL）适合此场景，但现有方法在保护已学知识（减少遗忘）的同时，需要兼顾通信和计算开销、公平性及可持续性，这些需求尚未得到有效满足。

Method: 提出FedCurv-DR策略：一种轻量级、基于正则化的联邦持续学习（FCL）方法。其核心思想是跨客户端和经验（任务）累积参数重要性估计来保护已学知识，并且仅在固定时间间隔更新这些重要性估计，从而最小化通信和计算开销。

Result: 在WikiArt图像数据集的风格分类持续学习场景中（包含不断演化的风格），FedCurv-DR在性能、能耗和公平性指标上进行了评估。结果表明该方法能够减少遗忘，并在性能、公平性和能效之间实现平衡，支持文化遗产领域的可持续AI。

Conclusion: FedCurv-DR为文化遗产场景下的联邦持续学习提供了一种兼顾知识保护与资源效率的可行方案，在减少灾难性遗忘的同时实现了性能、公平性和能效的平衡，为可持续AI在数字人文领域的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence can support cultural heritage and digital humanities through large-scale retrieval and analysis of digitized collections. However, cultural heritage data are often distributed across institutions, constrained by ownership and access restrictions, and continuously evolving over time. Federated Continual Learning (FCL) is well suited to this setting, as it enables models to learn from distributed and sequential data without sharing raw collections. In this paper, we propose FedCurv-DR, a lightweight, regularisation-based FCL strategy. The method accumulates parameter-importance estimates across clients and experiences to protect learned knowledge, while updating them only at fixed intervals to minimize communication and computation overhead. We evaluate FedCurv-DR in a continual learning scenario using the WikiArt image dataset for genre classification with evolving styles, reporting performance, energy, and fairness metrics. Our results show that FedCurv- DR reduces forgetting and balances performance, fairness, and energy efficiency for sustainable AI in cultural heritage.

</details>


### [278] [End-to-end Early Classification of Time Series in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20044)
*Aurélien Renault,Alexis Bondu,Antoine Cornuéjols,Vincent Lemaire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了早期时间序列分类（ECTS）中传统可分离设计（分类与触发独立优化）的范式，在非平稳/概念漂移条件下首次系统比较了可分离与端到端方法，并提出基于强化学习的统一架构DQeND，同时学习表征、分类和触发决策，在多种漂移场景下显著优于可分离基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECTS方法大多假设数据平稳，且采用可分离设计（分类与触发决策独立优化），这从根本上限制了其在概念漂移下的适应性。作者质疑这一范式，旨在探索非平稳条件下更鲁棒的ECTS方案。

Method: 提出DQeND（Deep Q-learning based end-to-end Network for Drift），一种基于强化学习（DQN）的统一架构，联合优化表征学习、分类与触发决策三个模块，同时保持与最先进可分离基线可直接比较的设定。此外设计了受控漂移场景用于系统对比，并进行消融实验。

Result: 在广泛的漂移场景下，DQeND展现出强鲁棒性，一致优于可分离基线。消融研究表明，联合更新表征与决策模块是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 端到端学习能够为动态环境中的ECTS提供更强的适应能力，研究结果为替代可分离设计提供了新的方向和证据，值得进一步深入探索。

Abstract: Early Classification of Time Series (ECTS) requires making accurate decisions as early as possible in inherently online and evolving environments. Yet, most existing methods assume stationarity and rely on separable designs, where classification and triggering are optimized independently, an assumption that fundamentally limits their adaptability under drift. In this work, we challenge this paradigm and study ECTS under non-stationary conditions. We provide the first systematic comparison between separable and end-to-end approaches across controlled drifting scenarios. Building on Reinforcement Learning, we introduce DQeND, a unified architecture that jointly learns representation, classification, and triggering decisions, while remaining directly comparable to state-of-the-art separable baselines. Across a wide range of drifts, DQeND demonstrates strong robustness across various non-stationary scenarios, consistently outperforming separable baselines. An ablation study further highlights that jointly updating representation and decision modules is critical to these gains. Overall, our results indicate that end-to-end learning can offer improved adaptation capabilities for ECTS in dynamic environments, and motivate further investigation of alternatives to separable designs.

</details>


### [279] [DecoVAE: a Lightweight Interpretable Trend-Seasonal VAE Framework for Efficient Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20052)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecoVAE，一个轻量级且可解释的趋势-季节变分自编码器框架，通过领域特定的归纳偏置显式分离时间序列的趋势与季节成分。趋势流使用微分正则化器强制结构平滑性（类比Hodrick-Prescott滤波器），季节流在频域通过复高斯VAE原生捕捉周期模式的振幅和相位。在七个真实世界基准上，DecoVAE在短期和长期预测中分别降低了最多14.96%/23.30%和52.68%/26.51%的误差指标，同时将模型权重减少93%、推理速度提升74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率时间序列预测中，趋势和季节成分具有不同的内在属性，现有方法难以同时捕捉两者的独特特性，要么缺乏可解释性，要么面临巨大的内存和运行时间开销。作者旨在解决这些问题，构建一个既能显式分解时间序列成分、又能保持高效率和可解释性的统一框架。

Method: DecoVAE采用双流结构：趋势流通过微分正则化器对潜在轨迹施加结构平滑性约束（类似于Hodrick-Prescott滤波器的工作方式）；季节流在频域中使用复高斯VAE，天然建模周期模式的振幅和相位。框架通过将领域专有的归纳偏置注入VAE架构，实现趋势与季节成分的显式分解。

Result: 在七个真实世界基准数据集上，DecoVAE持续优于强基线方法。短期预测中CRPS降低最多14.96%、NMAE降低23.30%；长期预测中分别降低52.68%和26.51%。同时模型权重最多减少93%，推理速度提升最多74%（相对次优方法）。

Conclusion: DecoVAE证明了一种轻量级、可解释的分解式VAE方法可以在概率时间序列预测中同时实现高精度和高效率，弥合了模型表达力与计算开销之间的鸿沟。趋势-季节分离的归纳偏置设计是提升预测性能和可解释性的有效途径。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting remains challenging, largely because modeling distinct trend and seasonal dynamics requires specialized approaches. Existing methods often fail to capture the unique inner properties of these components, lack interpretability, or suffer from heavy memory and runtime overhead. To address these limitations, we propose DecoVAE, a lightweight interpretable trend-seasonal VAE framework that explicitly decomposes time series into trend and seasonal components by applying domain-specific inductive biases. The trend stream enforces structural smoothness using a differential regularizer on the latent trajectory, analogous to the Hodrick-Prescott filter. Concurrently, the seasonal stream operates in the frequency domain via a complex Gaussian VAE, natively capturing the amplitude and phase of periodic patterns. Extensive evaluations across seven real-world benchmarks show that DecoVAE consistently outperforms strong baselines. It achieves reductions of up to 14.96\% in CRPS and 23.30\% in NMAE for short-term forecasting, and up to 52.68\% and 26.51\% for long-term horizons. Crucially, DecoVAE yields these accuracy gains while remaining highly efficient, reducing model weight by up to 93\% and accelerating speed by up to 74\% compared to the second-best method.

</details>


### [280] [Let's Scale Step by Step: Compute-Efficient Hyperparameter Transfer for Large-Scale Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20061)
*Nayeon Kim,Hojin Lee,Yunju Bak,Jaesun Park,Boseop Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种计算高效的两步超参数迁移框架，通过在小规模模型上获取最优学习率，先沿模型宽度维度和token维度迁移，最终预测大规模MoE模型在万亿token训练水平上的最优学习率（R²=0.95），并在155B参数（17B激活）的模型上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在扩展模型容量时，其超参数（尤其是学习率）在极端规模（模型大小和token预算）下通过穷举搜索来调优在计算上不可行。需要一种计算高效的方法来预测大规模训练的最优学习率。

Method: 两步超参数迁移框架：（1）基于Maximal Update Parameterization (μP)适配MoE架构（结合MLA和Muon优化器），验证最优学习率在宽度缩放模型间的可迁移性；（2）沿token维度建立预测性缩放定律，对小代理模型在有限预算下得到的最优学习率进行线性回归，外推至万亿token训练规模。

Result: 线性回归外推至10万亿token训练水平具有高保真度（R²=0.95）。将该方法应用于155B总参数（17B激活）的基础模型从零训练，训练稳定，评估结果验证了全规模目标模型的最优配置可以被准确预测。

Conclusion: 小模型上的代理训练足以确定大规模MoE模型广泛训练的最优学习率，以最小的消融代价准确预测全规模目标模型的最优配置。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures significantly expand model capacity without a proportional increase in computational cost. However, optimizing their hyperparameters---particularly the learning rate---at extreme scales of both model size and token budget via sweeping remains computationally prohibitive. In this paper, we propose a compute-efficient, two-step hyperparameter transfer framework that estimates optimal learning rates for training large MoE models by transferring them across scaling model widths, and subsequently extrapolating to trillion-token horizons. First, we formulate a Maximal Update Parameterization ($μ$P) adaptation for MoE architectures utilizing Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) and the Muon optimizer, demonstrating that optimal learning rates transfer consistently across width-scaled models. Second, we extend this transferability along the token dimension by establishing a predictive scaling law. By applying linear regression to the optimal values derived from small proxy models on limited budgets, we successfully extrapolate the ideal learning rate to massive training horizons (e.g., 10 trillion tokens) with high fidelity ($R^2=0.95$). Consequently, this indicates that proxy training on small models is sufficient to determine the optimal learning rate for the extensive training of large-scale MoEs. We apply the proposed methodology to pretrain our foundation model (155B total, 17B active parameters) from scratch, and the stable training and evaluation results validate that optimal configurations for full-scale target models can be accurately predicted with minimal ablation costs.

</details>


### [281] [Orthogonal JEPA: Factorized Predictive States for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20065)
*Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出\method，一种基于正交预测分解的潜在世界建模框架。通过学习基矩阵将目标状态分解为多个成分，每个成分由独立预测分支从共享上下文表示中估计，并配合多种正则化手段防止坍塌和冗余，实现对复杂系统的预测、规划与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）将所有可预测内容组织在一个目标嵌入和单一预测通路中，形成单一的整体状态。在复杂系统中，这种单一体结构会将冗余容量分配给主导信号，而对欠主导的预测结构提供较弱或相互冲突的梯度，导致表征质量下降。

Method: \method采用正交预测因式分解：学习到的基矩阵将每个目标状态分析为多个成分；每个成分由专门的预测分支从共享上下文表示中估计。预测回归保留状态合成所需的因式幅度，正交性目标防止重复方向，因子活性正则化维持投影目标的变异性，在线方差正则化防止坐标级编码器坍塌。预测成分可合成为完整潜在状态，供读出器、解码器、规划器或自回归展开使用。该机制统一适用于时间未来、空间隐藏或同系统部分观测等各类预测目标。

Result: 在受控视觉、单细胞转录组学、纵向健康记录、连续控制和分子动力学等实验中，评估了表征质量、预测、规划及长时程稳定性。实验表明\method在多个维度上的有效性优于标准JEPAs。

Conclusion: 正交预测因式分解为世界建模提供了一种避免单一整体状态瓶颈的机制，通过分解-预测-合成流程提升了复杂系统中潜在状态的学习质量，并在多种任务和数据类型上验证了其通用性和稳定性。

Abstract: World models construct latent states that support prediction, planning, and reasoning about an underlying system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) offer a direct way to learn such states by predicting targets in representation space instead of reconstructing every detail of the observation. Standard JEPAs, however, organize all predictable content through one target embedding and one prediction pathway. In complex systems, this monolithic state can allocate redundant capacity to dominant signals while providing weak or conflicting gradients to less dominant predictive structure. We introduce \method, a latent world-modeling framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization. Learned basis matrices analyze each target state into multiple components, and a dedicated prediction branch estimates each component from a shared context representation. Predictive regression preserves the factor magnitudes required for state synthesis, an orthogonality objective discourages repeated directions, factor-activity regularization maintains variation in projected targets, and online variance regularization discourages coordinate-wise encoder collapse. Predicted components are synthesized into a complete latent state that can be used by a readout, decoder, planner, or autoregressive rollout. The same predictive-state mechanism applies when the target is temporally future, spatially hidden, or another partial observation of the same system. Experiments on controlled vision, single-cell transcriptomics, longitudinal health records, continuous control, and molecular dynamics evaluate representation quality, forecasting, planning, and long-horizon stability.

</details>


### [282] [SAE-Xplainers: Rule-Based Feature Interpretation for Extreme Earth Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20117)
*Hugo Porta,Emanuele Dalsasso,Chang Xu,Theo Gnassounou,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于极端地球事件（ExEE）可解释性分析的方法，通过基于地理位置的SAE输入调制和规则化SAE-Xplainers集成，在火灾预测、热带气旋和大气河检测三个任务上实现了与科学文献一致的可解释规则提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模气象与气候数据集为深度学习建模极端地球事件提供了机遇，但其在业务化应用中的采用受到模型可解释性不足的限制。虽然SAE在文本和图像模态中已被证明能提取人类可理解的概念，但由于气象气候数据的特殊性（如空间异质性、多模态复杂性），直接将SAE用于极端事件分析仍面临挑战。

Method: 本文提出两个核心技术贡献：(i) 基于地理位置的信息调制方法，对SAE的输入进行空间感知调整，以捕获环境模式的局部语义含义；(ii) 一种基于规则的SAE-Xplainers集成方法，将多模态环境预测因子产生的高维特征展开为人类可理解的规则。

Result: 在火灾预测、热带气旋检测和大气河检测三类极端事件上的实验表明：(1) SAE输入调制同时改善了重建性能和特征利用率；(2) SAE-Xplainers能够忠实解释复杂的气候模式，将其展开为与科学文献一致的、人类可理解的规则；(3) 所提方法还支持特征吸收（feature absorption）的识别。

Conclusion: 本文通过地理位置调制的稀疏自编码器与规则化解释器集成，有效解决了极端地球事件深度模型的可解释性问题，为该领域从研究走向业务化应用迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: The emergence of large-scale Weather and Climate (W&C) datasets offers new opportunities for modeling extreme Earth events (ExEE) and their impacts using deep learning. However, their adoption in operational settings remains limited by the lack of models' interpretability. While for conventional text and image modalities, tools such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have proven effective for extracting human-understandable concepts, their use for the analysis of ExEE remains challenging due to the nature of W&C data. To address this, we introduce (i) a geographic location-based modulation of the inputs of SAE to capture the local semantic meaning of environmental patterns, and (ii) an ensemble of rule-based SAE-Xplainers to interpret the resulting high-dimensional features derived from complex, multi-modal environmental predictors. We evaluate our method on three ExEE types: the prediction of fires, and the detection of tropical cyclones and atmospheric rivers. We show that SAE input modulation improves both reconstruction performance and feature utilization, and that our SAE-Xplainers enable faithful interpretation of complex climatic patterns by unfolding them into human-understandable rules that are consistent with the scientific literature, while also supporting the identification of feature absorption.

</details>


### [283] [Evaluating Neural Cartographic Relief Shading for Urban Environments: A Downtown Calgary Study Using High-Resolution DEM and DSM Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20149)
*Emmanuel Stefanakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在加拿大卡尔加里市中心的高密度城市环境中，使用高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，系统比较了单方向/多方向解析山体阴影法与基于机器学习（Eduard系统）的神经网络山体阴影法。研究发现，虽然Eduard最初针对山地景观训练，但通过参数调优（地形类型、微/宏泛化、平坦区域细节等），在部分情形下能产生视觉上强烈且制图上有用的结果，甚至优于传统解析方法；同时在高密度建成区，其训练偏向阿尔卑斯地形的局限性也导致某些场景的失败。文章以探索性比较而非基准测试为定位，论证了未来应针对城市地形进行专门的神经着色模型训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的解析山体阴影法在城市环境中存在表达能力不足的问题，而基于机器学习的Eduard系统虽然在自然地形上表现优异，却从未被系统评估过其在城市建成环境中的适用性。本文的核心动机是检验一个为山地景观训练的神经模型是否通过参数调优就能适应高密度城市环境，从而为城市地形渲染提供新的技术路径。

Method: 采用探索性对比分析方法，在卡尔加里市中心的高分辨率DEM和DSM数据上，分别生成单方向解析山体阴影、多方向解析山体阴影以及Eduard神经网络山体阴影。在神经实验中保持大幅山体阴影风格常量，重点调节地形类型、微/宏泛化和平坦区域细节等参数，并进行系统的视觉与制图质量对比评估。

Result: 研究识别出了三类结果：解析山体阴影仍然更可靠的场景、Eduard展现出乎意料优势的场景、以及由于模型对阿尔卑斯地形的训练偏差而导致神经山体阴影失败的场景。参数调优能够在部分城市地物（如建筑、桥梁、街道等）上产生视觉上有竞争力甚至更优的着色结果，但并非普遍适用。

Conclusion: 研究证明了针对自然地貌训练的神经着色模型经过参数调优后，在部分城市环境中具有可用性和优势，但深层次的训练偏差限制了其整体表现。文章呼吁未来应专门针对城市地形进行神经模型的训练与评估，以弥补现有模型的领域鸿沟。

Abstract: This article explores the performance of analytical and neural-based hillshading methods in a dense urban environment using high-resolution digital elevation model (DEM) and digital surface model (DSM) data for downtown Calgary. The study compares single-direction and multi-direction analytical hillshading with relief shading generated in Eduard, a machine-learning system originally developed to emulate Swiss-style shaded relief trained primarily on mountainous landscapes. Because Eduard was not designed for buildings, bridges, streets, trees, and other urban infrastructures, the central question is not whether it perfectly reproduces urban morphology, but whether parameter tuning can nevertheless produce visually strong, cartographically useful, and in some cases superior results when compared with conventional analytical methods. The analysis focuses especially on terrain type, micro and macro generalization, and flat-area detail parameters, while keeping the large-scale shading style constant throughout the neural experiments. The article is structured as an exploratory comparison rather than a benchmark of universal best practice. It aims to identify where analytical hillshading remains more reliable, where Eduard offers unexpected strengths, and where neural shading fails because of its training bias toward alpine terrain. The study contributes to current work on terrain representation by testing whether a neural approach designed for natural landforms can be adapted to a highly built urban setting, and it concludes by arguing for future model training and evaluation specifically targeted at urban relief shading.

</details>


### [284] [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Relation，一种替代Attention的token混合原语，通过将两两证据组织成显式的Self和Exchange关系来推导信息流，并在多个模型规模上验证了其优于标准多头注意力（MHA）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Attention直接从两两分数推导归一化信息流，缺乏对token间关系结构的显式组织。作者认为先组织关系再推导信息流可能更有效，因此提出关系优先（relation-first）的token混合视角。

Method: 提出了Relation原语，将两两证据组织为显式的Self和Exchange两类关系，再从中推导信息流。在此基础上发展出多种变体：Full Relation（完整实现）、FlashRelation（高效实现）、Linear Relation（线性复杂度）、Hybrid Relation（混合使用Linear层），以及KV风格的Relation Cache（KV style缓存）。

Result: 在约10M、30M和100M参数的decoder-only模型上，Full Relation在所有三个规模下均取得比MHA更低的最终验证NLL。FlashRelation在固定上下文基准测试中比物化的Full Relation实现快3.60-4.41倍。在生产工作负载中，FlashRelation达到PyTorch FlashAttention吞吐量的76.4-84.9%（同时执行完整Full Relation算子）。Hybrid Relation采用75%的Linear Relation层，仍实现强语言建模质量。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持关系优先（relation-first）的token混合观点，即先问Self，再问Others，然后让Flow跟随Relation，为注意力机制的替代设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Attention directly derives normalized information flow from pairwise scores. We introduce Relation, an alternative token-mixing primitive that first organizes pairwise evidence into explicit Self and Exchange relations and derives information flow afterward. This relational organization gives rise to Full Relation, FlashRelation, Linear Relation, Hybrid Relation, and a KV-style Relation Cache. Across matched decoder-only models at approximately 10M, 30M, and 100M parameters, Full Relation achieves lower final validation NLL than MHA at all three scales. In a fixed-context reference benchmark, FlashRelation is 3.60-4.41x faster than the materialized Full Relation implementation. Across scale-matched production workloads, it reaches 76.4-84.9% of PyTorch FlashAttention throughput while executing the Full Relation operator. Hybrid Relation uses 75% Linear Relation layers and achieves strong language-modeling quality. These results support a relation-first view of token mixing: ask Self, ask Others, then let Flow follow Relation.

</details>


### [285] [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Christian Bergler,Johann Jäger,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向机器学习电力系统保护评估的标准化框架，定义七个必需的研究维度，并在PROTECT-90基准上实例化验证，证明评估设计本身应作为科学贡献的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习电力系统保护研究普遍报告近乎完美的分数，但分数含义强烈依赖于评估设置（任务、物理范围、测量、时序、目标、预处理、验证方式），这些因素常常联合变化且描述不完整，导致结果不可比较、难以审计，也无法支撑认证导向的评估。

Method: 提出标准化导向框架，定义七个必需研究维度：保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时序与决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议、评估输出。在PROTECT-90电磁暂态基准（含9022个模拟故障剧集）上实例化为有界案例研究，采用集中式感知、20ms仿真元数据对齐窗口及剧集分组验证，评估MLP分类与定位性能，并对比不同决策时域、可观测性退化及同步两端传统定位器。

Result: MLP在五折剧集分组验证下取得分类宏平均F1为0.991±0.001，定位平均绝对误差为线长的10.20±0.25%。决策窗口延长至50ms保持任务依赖的性能不对称；可观测性降低使定位误差约翻倍但对分类影响甚微。同步双端传统定位器在其更丰富的信息集下优于学习型定位器；测量退化显示干净条件下的预测性能不能决定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 框架将评估假设转化为显式可复现的证据，为机器学习保护功能提供更可比、可审计的评估基础，并支撑未来认证导向评估的标准化设计。

Abstract: Studies of machine-learning-based power-system protection increasingly report near-perfect scores, yet the meaning of those scores depends strongly on the evaluation setting. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. It defines seven required study dimensions: protection objective, physical scope, observability, timing and decision windows, targets and sample validity, validation protocol, and evaluation outputs. The framework is instantiated in a bounded case study on the public PROTECT-90 electromagnetic-transient benchmark, comprising 9022 simulated episodes from a 90 kV double-line topology, for onset-conditioned fault classification and localization. Under centralized sensing, simulation-metadata-aligned 20 ms windows, and episode-grouped validation, a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieved a five-fold mean macro-averaged F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.001 for classification and a localization mean absolute error of 10.20 +/- 0.25% of line length (mean +/- std across episode-grouped folds). Extending the decision horizon to 50 ms preserved this task-dependent performance asymmetry, while reduced observability approximately doubled the MLP localization error but had little effect on classification. A synchronized two-ended conventional locator outperformed the learning locators under its richer clean information set, and measurement degradation showed that clean predictive performance did not determine robustness. The framework turns evaluation assumptions into explicit, reproducible evidence and provides a basis for more comparable, auditable evaluation and future certification-oriented assessment of machine-learning protection functions.

</details>


### [286] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个确定性算法，能够精确计算任意二维模型（其KL散度接触等价于多项式）的局部实对数规范阈值（RLCT），并给出了复杂度界和广泛实验验证，包括多项式神经网络应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息准则（如BIC）在奇异模型（如深度学习）中因正则性假设不成立而失效。WBIC依赖局部学习系数（即局部RLCT）来捕捉正确的边际似然渐近行为，但精确计算这些系数一直局限于特殊情况，一般只能使用基于采样的估计方法。

Method: 提出首个确定性算法，对任意二维模型（KL散度接触等价于多项式）精确计算局部RLCT；推导算法复杂度上界；在广泛模型类别中验证有效性，包括多项式神经网络等应用。

Result: 算法在验证的模型类别中表现出色，在浅层（shallow）模型体制下不仅精确性优于采样方法，且计算速度更快；同时能揭示采样方法无法发现的代数结构信息。

Conclusion: 该确定性算法为校准基于采样的估计器提供了ground truth，并在浅层模型场景中展现出速度和精度上的双重优势，且能揭示学习系数中隐含的代数结构。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [287] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出数据知情质心分裂（DICS）框架，利用基于聚类的数据驱动先验构建紧凑的候选分裂集，在保持分类树预测性能的同时大幅降低训练时间，适用于决策树、随机森林和梯度提升模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树模型因其可解释性和强大的经验性能被广泛使用，但训练成本高昂，尤其是在大规模和高维数据集上，主要瓶颈是每个节点需要对候选分裂进行穷举搜索。作者希望通过数据驱动的先验来减少搜索空间，提高训练效率。

Method: DICS是一种基于聚类的框架，利用数据驱动的先验信息（特别是类别感知结构）构建一个紧凑且信息丰富的候选分裂集，从而显著缩小分类任务中的分裂搜索空间，同时保持预测性能。

Result: 理论分析表明在既定假设下，DICS相比穷举分裂搜索不会降低分类树的性能。大量实验证明，DICS在合成和基准数据集上能达到与穷举搜索相当的精度，同时大幅减少训练时间。

Conclusion: 将数据知情的先验信息整合到分裂选择中是实现可扩展分类树学习的一种有效策略，DICS为大规模决策树训练提供了一种高效且保持性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [288] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用PCMCI+算法从105例家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试（HSAT）记录中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，揭示了不同性别和年龄亚组之间因果结构的系统性差异。研究发现时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有队列中普遍存在，而其他因果关系则显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动态复杂且在不同患者群体间存在差异，阻碍了针对性干预措施的发展。理解不同亚组（性别、年龄）间因果结构的差异，有助于开发更精准的干预策略。

Method: 采用PCMCI+算法对来自105例HSAT记录的加窗分数变量进行因果学习；利用领域知识通过边缘黑名单（edge blacklisting）约束搜索空间，并采用自助聚合（bootstrap aggregation）以应对小子组样本量问题。

Result: 学习得到的因果图显示，时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有队列中持续存在，而其他因果关系在不同性别和年龄亚组之间表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果结构在不同患者人群间存在系统性差异，但核心的时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系具有普遍性，这为针对不同亚组设计差异化干预提供了因果层面的依据。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [289] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对字典学习后稀疏追踪在高度相干字典下物理定位不精确的问题，提出了一种分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，通过跨字典置信对应同时保留与校准数据和部署数据兼容的字典与稀疏表示，并引入自适应有限候选库算法（AEB）实现计算高效的物理定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在字典学习后的稀疏追踪中，即使能够精确获得原子支撑，其物理解释也可能不被校准数据所支持。尤其是对于高度相干的字典，存在多种与校准数据兼容的备选字典，它们可能将相同的支撑赋予不同的物理含义，导致物理定位的不确定性。这一问题亟需解决。

Method: 提出了分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法：(1) 联合建模字典学习不确定性与部署信号表示不确定性；(2) 设计跨字典置信对应，同时保留校准兼容字典和部署兼容稀疏表示，并将幸存解释投影到物理支撑空间；(3) 引入自适应端点括入（Active Endpoint Bracketing, AEB）算法，仅在可能影响物理报告的候选上进行评估，否则安全地粗化或弃权。

Result: 对于分离尺度为s的局部相干原子类，一旦部署数据解析了相干块解释及其原子支撑，来自N个校准信号的最小极值物理分辨率满足δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N s²)}，相对分辨率由方向信息尺度Ns⁶控制。部署复制仅当方向变化无法通过调整活动系数吸收时才改善物理定位。有限候选库实验（包括四区域合成应用）表明，点值插入式选择器可能物理上过精确，而AEB避免了无依据的细化且候选评估更少。

Conclusion: 所提出的分辨率感知物理支撑推断方法能够更准确地刻画物理定位的不确定性，AEB算法在计算效率和物理可靠性之间取得了良好平衡，避免了点值估计器可能产生的物理上过度精确的误导性结果。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [290] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BERT-LER，一种基于BERT风格的EHR时间线预测模型，将实验室检测结果编码为离散token并保留分级信息（基于百分位的分箱），结合Integrated Gradients实现token级可解释性，在75百万患者数据上预训练，在EHRShot基准和哮喘严重程度进展任务上取得有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化EHR预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息和针对输入医疗事件的可解释性，存在方法论空白。

Method: 采用BERT风格的架构，将实验室检测结果离散化为token并通过百分位分箱保留分级信息；使用去标识化75百万患者EHR数据集进行预训练和微调；结合Integrated Gradients进行token级归因分析。

Result: 在EHRShot基准和哮喘严重程度进展研究中，BERT-LER的预测性能与公开基准模型竞争，在实验室相关任务上常超过基准；归因结果与已知临床风险因素一致。

Conclusion: BERT-LER统一了实验室值表示与可解释性，在标准临床预测任务之外验证了预测性能和解释能力的可泛化性，可应用于许多治疗领域和基于结构化EHR语言模型的预测任务。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [291] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对天花板安装的FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi感知三种射频技术在相同部署条件下进行了受控对比实验，使用20名受试者在6种房间布局下的同步记录数据，以相同CNN模型评估10类细粒度HAR任务和4类粗粒度睡眠监测任务。结果显示IR-UWB在跨受试者行为识别上最优（89.0%宏F1），FMCW对未见房间布局泛化最好（83.8%宏F1），睡眠监测任务所有技术均超过92%宏F1，揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频医疗健康监测研究（FMCW雷达、IR-UWB、Wi-Fi感知）通常使用不同硬件、数据集和评估方法，缺乏在相同部署条件下的公平比较；同时天花板安装雷达虽具有实际部署和成本优势，但性能未被充分探索。因此需要一项受控对比研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用受控实验设计：在20名参与者、6种房间布局下使用同步记录的方式采集FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三种射频技术的传感数据；所有技术使用相同的卷积神经网络（CNN）架构进行公平评估；既包括细粒度的10类人类活动识别（HAR）任务，也包括粗粒度的4类睡眠监测任务。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中取得最高性能（89.0%宏F1）；FMCW在未见房间布局的泛化上表现最佳（83.8%宏F1）；睡眠监测中所有技术均超过92%宏F1（未见环境）。三种技术在识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间表现出明显的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 不同射频技术在识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性权衡，这种权衡可通过距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留能力的差异来解释。研究为面向医疗保健的射频感知系统设计提供了实用指导原则。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [The Lazy Pod That Lies: Deferred Cost and Failure Semantics of Lazy Container Image Pulling for Model Serving on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19412)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了Kubernetes上KServe平台中两种生产级懒加载镜像拉取系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter与AWS SOCI）在模型分发场景下的表现。懒加载确实实现了冷启动首预测时间与镜像大小无关（16.9-17.6秒 vs 急切拉取的24.5-573.0秒），但代价被延迟而非消除：完整读取14GB模型耗时105.3秒，慢于急切拉取的72.4秒。更严重的是，懒加载暴露了急切拉取不可能出现的失败模式——节点级缓存耗尽导致正在运行的pod读取模型文件失败，且存在快照守护进程重启后文件句柄永久失效的严重问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型服务pod的启动成本主要由容器镜像拉取主导，懒加载承诺通过"立即挂载+按需拉取"消除这一成本。然而，该承诺在真实生产环境模型分发场景下的实际收益与代价尚未被系统评估，特别是对于大体积模型权重（真实fp16权重，2-140GB范围）和长时间运行的服务生命周期。

Method: 在Kubernetes上使用KServe平台，针对2至140GB（含真实fp16权重）的模型工件，将两种生产级懒加载系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI）与急切拉取基线进行对比实验。测量冷启动首预测时间、完整读取性能、系统生命周期开销分配，并系统性地刻画缓存耗尽下的失败模式及故障恢复行为。

Result: 懒加载实现了冷启动首预测时间与大小无关（16.9-17.6s vs 24.5-573.0s急切）。但完整读取14GB模型耗时105.3s，慢于急切拉取的72.4s。两系统在生命周期两端支付代价：SOCI在Ready前几乎预取完整镜像，eStargz将所有内容推迟到首次读取。缓存耗尽时，67-94%的模型文件失败，且存在196秒的无症状故障窗口；快照守护进程重启会导致存活pod的文件句柄永久失效而pod仍显示Running。

Conclusion: 懒加载兑现了冷启动速度的核心承诺，但将成本推迟而非消除，并引入了急切拉取在结构上不可能出现的失败模式。建议服务平台与运维人员关注节点级缓存容量规划、故障监控（特别是无症状故障窗口）以及快照守护进程生命周期管理，并为容器平台运维提供实例化的放置、监控与缓存容量设计指南。

Abstract: Lazy container-image pulling promises to eliminate the dominant cost of starting a model-serving pod by mounting the image immediately and fetching content on demand. We evaluate this promise for model delivery on Kubernetes, using KServe with two production lazy-pulling systems -- eStargz/stargz-snapshotter and AWS SOCI -- against eager baselines, on artifacts from 2 to 140 GB including real fp16 weights. Lazy pulling delivers its headline: cold time-to-first-prediction becomes size-independent (16.9--17.6s, versus 24.5--573.0s eager). But the cost is deferred, not eliminated: a full read of a 14 GB model through the lazy mount takes 105.3s, slower than the 72.4s eager pull it replaced, and the two systems pay at opposite lifecycle ends (SOCI prefetches nearly the full image before Ready; eStargz defers nearly everything to first read). More consequentially, we characterize a failure mode eager pulling structurally cannot exhibit: under sustained legitimate reads with default configuration, the snapshotter's node-level cache exhausts its finite volume and already-running pods begin failing reads of model files. At the earliest stage of exhaustion, an instrumented serving pod passed every Kubernetes-visible and application-level check for 196s while its snapshotter was already logging real failures; under heavier pressure, 67--94% of model files fail, scaling monotonically with residual cache occupancy. A live pod self-heals if cache space is freed under it, but a snapshotter-daemon restart under a live pod leaves permanently stale file handles in a pod still reported Running. We derive placement, monitoring, and cache-sizing guidance for serving platforms and operators.

</details>


### [293] [When Do LLM Agents Help? Deadline-Aware Mixed-Criticality Task Scheduling at the Autonomous-Vehicle Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19557)
*Reza Zakerian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究异构MEC服务器上的截止时间感知、混合关键性调度问题。作者首先构建了一个窗口化合同网拍卖启发式算法，在60个实例上达到0.902的关键任务完成率，优于所有基线。随后引入多智能体LLM控制平面，通过受控分解发现：在静态负载下启发式已接近最优，LLM无增益；但在任务激增的非平稳场景下，LLM控制平面显著优于静态启发式与bandit方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆将时延敏感的感知任务卸载到移动边缘计算服务器，安全关键任务的错过不可接受。虽然LLM被越来越多地提议作为自适应、可解释的调度器，但缺乏其何时真正有效的证据。作者希望严格评估LLM控制平面相对于强启发式方法是否带来实质性改进，并明确其适用的条件。

Method: 两步方法：第一步构建启发式基线——窗口化合同网拍卖算法，按最早截止时间在每接入窗口内对关键任务优先排序，并按最早完成时间放置任务；第二步添加多智能体LLM控制平面，通过受控分解将调度器优势追溯至批量窗口大小和关键任务优先排序两个因素。在静态负载与中段安全关键任务激增两种场景下分别评估。

Result: 启发式算法在60个实例、三种拓扑、15个基线的对比中达到TC完成率0.902，优于所有基线（Holm校正p<0.001，最佳基线0.838），达到CP-SAT上界的0.87。在静态负载下，LLM控制平面无增益，拍卖、逐窗口LLM策略和在线自适应均无贡献；在任务激增场景下，LLM控制平面显著优于静态启发式和bandit。

Conclusion: LLM编排只有在非平稳性为固定策略无法利用的情况打开空间时，才值得其计算成本。控制平面的延迟和推理依据已报告，所有代码和带种子的实例已公开。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offload latency-sensitive perception tasks to nearby mobile edge computing (MEC) servers, where a missed safety-critical task is unsafe rather than merely degraded. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as adaptive, explainable schedulers, yet evidence of when they help is scarce. We study deadline-aware, mixed-criticality scheduling on heterogeneous MEC servers, where time-critical (TC) tasks must be protected at a controlled cost to best-effort traffic, and ask whether a multi-agent LLM control layer improves on a strong heuristic. We answer in two steps. First we build the heuristic: a windowed contract-net auction that orders each admission window time-critical-first by earliest deadline and places tasks by earliest-finish-time. Across 60 instances on three topologies and 15 baselines under an identical online constraint, it attains a TC completion rate of 0.902, above every baseline (Holm-corrected p < 0.001; best baseline 0.838) and at 0.87 of a CP-SAT upper bound. Second, we add the LLM control plane. A controlled decomposition traces the scheduler's advantage to two ordinary factors, the batching horizon and time-critical-first ordering; the auction, the per-window LLM policy, and online adaptation add nothing while the load is stationary, where the heuristic is already near-optimal. Under a mid-run surge of safety-critical tasks the picture changes, and the LLM control plane gains significantly over both the static heuristic and the bandit. LLM orchestration therefore earns its cost only when non-stationarity opens headroom a fixed policy cannot use. We report control-plane latency and rationale, and release all code and seeded instances.

</details>


### [294] [Optimality and Trade-offs in Fast BFT SMR (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19629)
*Neil Giridharan,Minh Tung Nguyen,Ittai Abraham,Allen Clement,Natacha Crooks,Pierre Sutra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对快速拜占庭容错（Fast BFT）状态机复制（SMR）协议所需的复制因子给出了严格的上界和下界，并提出了一个次优但能体现复制因子与恢复效率之间权衡的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速SMR协议在崩溃故障（crash-fault）场景下已得到充分研究，并因其低执行延迟、高吞吐量、高可用性和公平性等优势受到学术界和工业界的广泛关注。然而，在拜占庭容错（BFT）场景下，快速SMR协议的研究还很不充分，仅有少量已有协议。因此，需要系统性地研究Fast BFT SMR所需的复制因子界限。

Method: 本文通过理论分析推导出Fast BFT SMR协议所需复制因子的严格上界和下界，并在此基础上设计了一个次优协议，用于展示复制因子与恢复效率之间的权衡关系。

Result: 给出了Fast BFT SMR协议复制因子的紧致上界与下界，并提出一个次优协议来演示复制因子与恢复效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文填补了Fast BFT SMR在理论界限方面的空白，为后续快速拜占庭容错协议的设计提供了理论指导，同时揭示了复制因子与恢复效率之间不可兼得的权衡关系。

Abstract: Fast state-machine replication (SMR) protocols in the crash-fault setting have attracted significant interest in both academia and industry. This interest stems from their advantages over leader-based protocols, including low execution latency for non-conflicting commands, high throughput, improved availability, and increased fairness. While Fast SMR is well studied in the crash-fault setting, the Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) setting remains much less understood, with only a small number of existing Fast BFT SMR protocols. In this paper, we present tight upper and lower bounds on the replication factor required for Fast BFT SMR. We also present a suboptimal protocol that illustrates a trade-off between replication factor and recovery efficiency.

</details>


### [295] [CacheRoute: Planned Prefix-Affinity Routing for Large-Scale LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19677)
*Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheRoute是一种基于周期性路由计划的KV缓存路由系统，通过将高频率key分配给稳定的热集合并依据期望负载放置，在兼顾亲和性（缓存复用）与负载均衡的同时，相比五种基线方法将吞吐量提升至2.3倍，并将KV缓存命中率从64.1%提升至93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存（prefix caching）仅在重复请求回到仍持有前缀KV的服务器时才避免prefill。传统的缓存无感知（cache-blind）负载均衡会分散缓存复用，而固定亲和性（fixed affinity）虽然保留复用却可能导致单服务器过载。这两种方法无法同时兼顾缓存利用率与负载均衡。

Method: CacheRoute通过周期性路由规划解决缓存亲和性与负载均衡之间的权衡：(1) 将高请求速率（high-rate）的key准入到一个稳定的热集合（warm set）；(2) 依据期望负载进行key到服务器的分配；(3) 允许热key拥有多个目标服务器以提高灵活性（虽然主聚合实验中每个key仍使用单一目标）。

Result: 在60块H100 GPU上运行Llama-3.3-70B（fp8精度）时，CacheRoute在3.5秒p99 SLO下维持176±11 QPS，是五种基线方法中最强者的2.3倍。KV缓存命中率从缓存无感知均衡下的64.1±1.3%提升至93.2±0.5%。通过第二个半合成聚合负载以及受控的8B与突发实验，分离了亲和性与放置各自的效果。但两个32B负载提供了反例：当亲和性恢复的KV工作量过少时，残余负载偏斜会削弱甚至消除改进。

Conclusion: 建议任何实际部署都应以影子回放（shadow replay）进行门控验证，而非仅凭工作负载统计启用亲和性，因为亲和性带来的收益取决于具体工作负载的KV复用程度。

Abstract: Prefix caching avoids prefill only when a repeated request returns to a server that still holds the prefix KV. Cache-blind balancing disperses that reuse; fixed affinity preserves it but can overload a server. CacheRoute resolves this tradeoff with a periodic routing plan. It admits high-rate keys to a stable warm set and places their assignments by expected load. Hot keys may use more than one destination, although every key in our primary semi-synthetic aggregate uses exactly one. On Llama-3.3-70B in fp8 across 60 H100 GPUs, CacheRoute sustains 176+/-11 QPS at a 3.5-s p99 SLO, 2.3x the strongest of five baselines. Served KV-cache hit rate rises from 64.1+/-1.3% under cache-blind balancing to 93.2+/-0.5%. A second semi-synthetic aggregate and controlled 8B and burst experiments separate the effects of affinity and placement. Two 32B workloads provide the counterexamples: when affinity recovers too little KV work, its residual load skew reduces or erases the improvement. We therefore recommend gating any deployment with a shadow replay rather than enabling affinity from workload statistics alone.

</details>


### [296] [ParaWeb: Parallel Programming Patterns for Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19935)
*Suejb Memeti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ParaWeb是一个TypeScript库，实现了十种并行编程模式，每种模式提供三种变体：基于postMessage的消息传递（MP）、基于SharedArrayBuffer的共享缓冲（Shared）和基于WebGPU的GPU变体。实验表明CPU变体在16线程下对计算密集型模式最高加速11.6倍，GPU变体最高加速260倍，图像卷积案例中GPU加速可达414倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用需要大量计算密集型处理，但JavaScript传统上受限于单线程执行模型。Node.js Worker Threads和浏览器Web Workers虽然提供了低层并行机制，但开发者缺乏捕获重复并行结构的高层抽象，无法将其作为可复用模式使用。

Method: ParaWeb库在每个模式上提供三种实现变体：MP变体使用structured cloning实现消息传递并行；Shared变体使用SharedArrayBuffer和typed array视图实现共享内存并行；GPU变体使用WebGPU计算着色器实现硬件加速。共实现30个模式的实例，覆盖服务端Node.js、客户端浏览器和WebGPU环境。

Result: CPU变体在16线程下对计算密集型模式获得最高11.6倍加速；GPU变体对高算术强度的Farm、Scatter、Reduce和Map等模式获得最高260倍加速。五个图像卷积滤波器的案例研究显示，在非可分离核上GPU加速可达414倍，并在1024x1024、2048x2048和4K图像上保持一致扩展性。

Conclusion: ParaWeb提供了统一的并行编程模式抽象层，覆盖CPU多线程和GPU加速两种路径，证明了高级并行模式可以以可复用模式的形式高效地在Web生态中实现，且GPU变体对高算术强度任务具有压倒性性能优势。

Abstract: Modern web applications increasingly require computationally intensive processing, yet JavaScript, the dominant language of the web, has traditionally been limited to a single-threaded execution model. Node.js Worker Threads and browser Web Workers provide low-level mechanisms for parallel execution, but developers lack high-level abstractions that capture recurring parallel structures as reusable patterns. In this paper, we present ParaWeb, a TypeScript library that implements ten parallel programming patterns for server-side Node.js, client-side browser environments, and WebGPU compute shaders. ParaWeb provides three implementation variants for each pattern: a message-passing (MP) variant based on structured cloning via postMessage, a shared-buffer (Shared) variant that uses SharedArrayBuffer with typed array views, and a GPU variant that uses WebGPU compute shaders for hardware-accelerated execution. We describe the architecture, design decisions, and pattern-specific implementation strategies, and we evaluate the performance of all thirty implementations across three data sizes. Experimental evaluation results show that the CPU-based variants achieve speedups of up to 11.6x with 16 threads for compute-bound patterns, while the GPU variants reach speedups of up to 260x for compute-bound patterns with high arithmetic intensity such as Farm, Scatter, Reduce, and Map. A case study on five image-convolution filters further shows that GPU acceleration reaches up to 414x speedup over single-threaded CPU on non-separable kernels, with consistent scaling across 1024x1024$, 2048x2048$, and 4K images.

</details>


### [297] [Design and Empirical Evaluation of a Network-Centric, On-Premises Architecture for Earth Observation Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20283)
*João Pinelo,João Gonçalves,Denis Willett,Amit Ruhela,Derek Steinmoeller,Uriel Mendoza,Pelumi S. Alao,Ronald Soares Lopes,Rogerio Atem de Carvalho,Pedro Mattos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的、以网络为中心的本地（on-premises）地球观测（EO）数据访问架构，并在其首个实际部署——AIR数据中心（大西洋云的创始节点）上进行了评估。系统由MinIO对象存储集群（100 GbE网络）、PostGIS元数据目录和OGC API-EDR访问层组成。研究发现网络带宽是批量EO数据访问存储吞吐量的主导约束，但存在阈值（该硬件类别下单服务器约10 Gbps）：低于阈值时网络容量决定交付能力，高于阈值后端点内存拓扑而非容量决定可用的带宽，进一步网络投资回报取决于端点内存配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测项目产生的数据量超过大多数机构网络的传输和存储能力。公共云平台适用于资源充足的机构，但大西洋盆地各机构面临连接性、主权和资金约束，使本地基础设施成为唯一可行路径。云原生数据格式虽支持高效部分读取，但其性能依赖底层网络带宽，而这一依赖关系很少被单独测量。

Method: 构建可复现的、以网络为中心的本地EO数据访问架构，包括MinIO对象存储集群（100 GbE网络织物）、PostGIS元数据目录和OGC API-EDR访问层。在持续并行负载下表征网络织物性能，评估EO代表性工作负载的对象存储吞吐量，并在相同硬件上对比节流基线（仅改变网络带宽这一变量）。还通过与合作机构的多站点复制基准测试来表征联邦原语。

Result: 网络带宽在阈值之前是批量EO数据访问存储吞吐量的主导约束；超过阈值后，端点内存拓扑而非容量决定系统能使用的带宽量。对于该硬件类别，阈值位于每台服务器10 Gbps以上。低于阈值时网络容量单独决定设施可交付能力，高于阈值后进一步网络投资的回报取决于可延迟采购的端点内存配置。

Conclusion: 该架构为受资源约束的机构提供了可行的本地EO数据访问方案，并通过精确隔离网络带宽变量，揭示了网络带宽与存储吞吐量之间的量化关系及阈值效应，为硬件投资决策提供了明确指导：低带宽环境先投资网络，高带宽环境则应关注端点内存拓扑。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) programmes generate data at volumes that exceed the transfer and storage capacity of most institutional networks. Public cloud platforms address this for well-resourced organisations, but institutions across the Atlantic basin face constraints in connectivity, sovereignty and funding that make on-premises infrastructure the only viable path. Cloud-native data formats enable efficient partial reads, yet their performance depends on the bandwidth of the underlying network fabric, a dependency rarely measured in isolation. This paper presents a replicable, network-centric architecture for on-premises EO data access, evaluated at its first operational deployment: the AIR Data Centre, founding node of the Atlantic Cloud. The system comprises a MinIO object storage cluster on a 100 GbE fabric, a PostGIS metadata catalogue and an OGC API-EDR access layer. We characterise the fabric under sustained parallel load, evaluate object storage throughput for EO-representative workloads, and compare measured performance against throttled baselines on identical hardware, isolating network bandwidth as the sole variable. Multi-site replication benchmarks with partner institutions characterise the federation primitive the model depends on. Network bandwidth is the dominant constraint on storage throughput for bulk EO data access up to a threshold; beyond it, endpoint memory topology rather than capacity governs how much bandwidth a system can use. For this hardware class that threshold lies above 10 Gbps per server. Below it, network capacity alone sets what the facility can deliver; above it, the return on further network investment depends on endpoint memory provisioning, which can be deferred and bought later.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [298] [HyperCut: Fast Inter-Layer Scheduling via Directed Hypergraph and Early Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19296)
*Ziang Wei,Zirui Xu,Sufeng Guo,Chuanchao Gao,Yiyang Gao,Arvind Easwaran,Yuxiang Fu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperCut，一个基于超图划分的分层划分-映射框架，通过将层间调度与层内调度的代价紧密耦合，将设计空间从O(9.899^N)压缩到O(N)，在10个案例上实现2.0倍性能提升和80.47%探索时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层间调度器需要等待完整的细粒度层内调度解析后才能获得代价反馈，这种解耦流程反复探索次优甚至不可行的层间调度方案，且缺乏早期剪枝机制，成为DNN编译器设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 关键观察是层内调度代价可以被层间切割固定了子网格形状后紧上界约束。基于此，提出HyperCut框架：利用有向超图(DHG)抽象DNN，引入统一表示State同时编码DHG划分、瓦片网格分配和张量批次切分，将划分与映射耦合为统一优化对象，实现层间调度的早期过滤。

Result: 对于N层的DNN，理论设计空间从SOTA开源调度器SET的O(9.899^N)降低到O(N)。在10个评估案例上，HyperCut相比SET基线实现2.0倍性能提升和80.47%探索时间减少（几何均值）。

Conclusion: HyperCut通过层级划分-映射框架有效地将层间与层内调度耦合，利用紧上界代价实现早期剪枝，显著缩小设计空间并提升DNN编译器在瓦片加速器上的调度效率。

Abstract: As deep neural networks (DNNs) continue to scale, inter-layer scheduling, which orchestrates the spatial allocation of compute resources and the temporal execution order across layers, has become a decisive factor in sustaining high utilization and energy efficiency on tiled accelerators. However, existing inter-layer schedulers defer cost feedback until a complete fine-grained intra-layer scheduling has been resolved. The resulting decoupled flow repeatedly explores sub-optimal or even infeasible inter-layer schedules, and the absence of early pruning during the inter-layer phase remains a critical bottleneck for design-space exploration (DSE) in DNN compilers.
  Our key observation is that the cost of an intra-layer scheduling can be tightly upper-bounded once the inter-layer cut fixes the sub-mesh shape, which lets us cost every inter-layer candidate without solving the intra-layer problem. Hence, we propose a hierarchical partitioning-and-mapping framework, HyperCut, that enables early filtering of inter-layer schedules based on hypergraph partitioning. Based on the directed hypergraph (DHG) abstraction of DNN, we introduce a unified representation, State, that jointly encodes the DHG partition, tile mesh allocation and tensor batch splitting. Thereby, partitioning and mapping are coupled into a union optimization object. For a DNN with N layers, the resulting theoretical design space is bounded by O(N), compared with O(9.899^N) for the state-of-the-art open-source scheduler SET. Across 10 evaluated cases, HyperCut achieves 2.0x performance improvement and 80.47% exploration time reduction over the SET baseline, measured by geometric mean.

</details>


### [299] [Performance Verification of the AmpereOne CPU Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19300)
*Doa'a Al-Otoom,Nicholas Kelly,Aaron Lindsay,Agreen Ahmadi,Ananth Kumar,Anthony Faubert,Benjamin Chaffin,Bret Toll,Eric Wallace,Lucas Crowthers,Mark Charney,Michael Chin,Michael Spradling,Scott Witscher,Sriyash Caculo,Syed Fakhri,Vivek Kothapalli,William Freelove,Mahesh Madhav*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AmpereOne定制CPU核心四代产品中采用的工业级性能验证（PV）方法论，以RTL设计与trace驱动的性能模型之间的周期精确相关性为核心，结合数据驱动的工作负载策划、高频日常回归系统和统一事件流分析框架，并通过分支预测单元和L2预取器的案例研究进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工艺技术缩放放缓，微架构创新成为性能提升的主要驱动力，这使得硅前性能验证（PV）变得空前关键。为了在流片前发现并避免代价高昂的性能缺陷，需要一套系统化、可迭代的工业级验证方法论。

Method: 该方法论以RTL设计对trace驱动性能模型的周期精确相关性为核心，包含三大支柱：数据驱动的工作负载策划、高频日常回归测试系统、以及统一事件流分析框架。验证策略采用层次化方法——先对单个单元（如分支预测单元、L2预取器）进行聚焦相关性验证，再推进到全核级验证。

Result: 通过四代AmpereOne定制CPU核心的实际应用，结合分支预测单元和L2预取器的案例研究，证明了这种纪律化、迭代式的验证流程对避免昂贵的硅后错误和确保复杂处理器达到性能目标至关重要。

Conclusion: 该验证方法论在工业规模上被证明有效且不可或缺，文章最后展望了微处理器行业中性能验证的未来发展方向。

Abstract: As process technology scaling slows, microarchitectural innovation has become the primary driver of performance gains, making pre-silicon Performance Verification (PV) more critical than ever. This paper presents the industrial-scale PV methodology applied across four generations of the AmpereOne custom CPU core, centered on the cycle-accurate correlation of the RTL design against a trace-driven performance model. The methodology integrates data-driven workload curation, a high-frequency daily regression system, and a unified event-stream framework for analysis. We demonstrate this methodology through case studies of the Branch Prediction Unit and L2 Prefetcher, highlighting a hierarchical strategy that first isolates individual units for focused correlation before proceeding to full-core verification. The results demonstrate that this disciplined, iterative process is indispensable for avoiding costly post-silicon bugs and ensuring complex processors meet their performance targets. We end with a look towards the future of PV in the microprocessor industry.

</details>


### [300] [HYDRA: A Heterogeneous Chiplet DSE Framework for Serving Dynamic Hybrid LLM Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19395)
*Jiahao Lin,Alish Kanani,Sangwan Lee,Jaehyun Park,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HYDRA是一个面向异构chiplet系统上混合Transformer-Mamba大语言模型（LLM）服务的综合性设计空间探索框架，联合优化芯片组合、布局、带宽配置、动态批处理和运行时调度，相比现有基线平均提升1.55倍吞吐量并降低43.7%的首令牌延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合Transformer-Mamba LLM虽然提升长上下文效率，但其异构计算和通信模式使得硬件加速困难。Chiplet架构虽可扩展，但静态架构配置与动态运行时策略组成的联合设计空间过于庞大，难以穷举探索。

Method: HYDRA联合探索五个关键维度：chiplet组成、放置、片间带宽配置、动态批处理和运行时调度。框架集成通信感知放置、动态批处理、弹性任务调度，并采用基于马尔可夫链的快速性能估计器来捕捉多租户运行时动态，实现高效且准确的探索。

Result: 在所有工作负载上，HYDRA平均比最先进基线提升1.55倍吞吐量，平均降低43.7%的首令牌延迟（TTFT），吞吐量增益最高可达2.3倍。

Conclusion: 架构与运行时策略的协同设计对于在异构chiplet系统上实现高效的大规模LLM服务至关重要，HYDRA验证了这种联合设计方法的有效性。

Abstract: Hybrid Transformer-Mamba large language models (LLMs) enhance long-context efficiency, but their heterogeneous computation and communication patterns complicate efficient hardware acceleration. Chiplet-based architectures offer a scalable solution by integrating specialized compute and memory units. However, the design space spanning static architectural configurations and dynamic runtime policies is prohibitively large to explore exhaustively. To address this challenge, we present HYDRA, a comprehensive design space exploration framework for hybrid LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems. HYDRA jointly explores chiplet composition, placement, inter-chiplet bandwidth provisioning, dynamic batching, and runtime scheduling. It integrates communication-aware placement, dynamic batching, elastic task scheduling, and a fast Markov-based performance estimator that captures multi-tenant runtime dynamics for efficient and accurate exploration. Across all workloads, HYDRA delivers 1.55x the throughput and 43.7 percent lower time-to-first-token on average, with throughput gains reaching up to 2.3x compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results highlight that co-designing architecture and runtime policies is critical for efficient large-scale LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems.

</details>


### [301] [Exact Multistate Reliability and Upgrade Design for Heterogeneous HBM Systems via Threshold-Pruned BAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19471)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个精确的多状态可靠性分析框架，用于HBM存储系统中部分服务（降带宽）情形的可靠性评估。该框架将每个服务单元建模为携带任意有限带宽状态集的多状态组件，可靠性定义为聚合带宽满足需求的概率。文章提出阈值剪枝多状态二叉加法树（TP-mBAT）算法，用于精确单阈值评估，并与动态规划方法进行对比，同时给出二进制—多状态映射的闭式误差关系及灵敏度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBM系统不仅存在完全服务或完全隔离，还可能出现部分服务（降带宽），即控制器可见的服务单元可提供完整、降低或零带宽，原因包括子通道隔离、通道重映射或保护开销。现有二进制k-out-of-n模型无法刻画这种多状态带宽退化现象，因此需要发展一个更一般化的多状态可靠性框架来精确建模此类系统。

Method: (1) 建立精确多状态可靠性框架，每个服务单元携带任意有限带宽状态集，可靠性为聚合带宽满足需求的概率；二进制k-out-of-n模型为其特例，并推出闭式二进制映射误差关系以量化平均带宽失真及提供简化筛选测试。(2) 提出阈值剪枝多状态二叉加法树（TP-mBAT）算法用于精确单阈值评估，该算法采用特定的"regime"设计：在紧凑公共网格上固定网格动态规划更优，在不可通约基准下TP-mBAT与携带相同阈值规则的动态规划对比。(3) 提出精确概率转移灵敏度分析，识别质量从退化状态转移到更高带宽状态何时改变系统成功概率。(4) 引入保留单元地板模型，允许第三种精确剪枝规则。(5) 提出潜在包状态混合模型以捕获共享应力依赖。

Result: 在16单元可通约控制实验中，剪枝DP需要273次更新，而TP-mBAT需要362,506次节点访问。在14单元不可通约基准上，TP-mBAT与动态规划扩展相同的状态空间（6,862节点对比6,861更新），但保留状态仅有17条遍历条目对比412,121个概率状态（在中心需求处），峰值存储从25.02 MB降至1,152 B。忽略依赖性的潜在包状态混合模型会使可靠性高估8.73个百分点。

Conclusion: 本文的多状态可靠性框架为HBM部分服务现象提供了精确建模工具。TP-mBAT算法在不可通约场景下通过极大压缩保留状态量实现显著存储节省，满足"不削弱基线"的公平对比要求。敏感性分析与保留地板剪枝规则进一步增强了框架的适用性，而潜在共享应力模型表明忽略依赖会显著高估可靠性，凸显了建模依赖关系的必要性。

Abstract: High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) systems can exhibit partial service rather than only full service or complete isolation: a controller-visible service unit may deliver full, reduced, or zero bandwidth because of sub-channel isolation, lane remapping, or protection overhead. This paper develops an exact multistate reliability framework in which each service unit carries an arbitrary finite set of bandwidth states and reliability is the probability that aggregate delivered bandwidth meets a demand. The binary k-out-of-n model is recovered as a special case, while closed-form binary-mapping error relations quantify mean-bandwidth distortion and provide a screening test for the simpler abstraction. For exact single-threshold evaluation a threshold-pruned multistate Binary-Addition-Tree (TP-mBAT) algorithm is proposed. It is deliberately regime-specific: fixed-grid dynamic programming is preferable on a compact common grid, where a 16-unit commensurate control required 273 pruned-DP updates versus 362,506 TP-mBAT node visits. On a reproducible 14-unit incommensurate benchmark, TP-mBAT is compared against a dynamic program carrying the same threshold rules, so that no baseline is weakened. Both expand the same state space, 6,862 nodes against 6,861 updates, and the separation lies entirely in retained state: 17 traversal entries against 412,121 probability states at central demand, reducing measured peak storage from 25.02 MB to 1,152 B. An exact probability-transfer sensitivity identifies when moving mass from a degraded state to a higher-bandwidth state changes system success, and a reserved-unit floor model admits a third exact pruning rule that is vacuous without such floors. A latent package-state mixture captures shared stress, where ignoring dependence overstates reliability by 8.73 percentage points.

</details>


### [302] [A Thread-Register Decoupled GPU Execution Model for Efficient Tensor Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19628)
*Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yangjie Zhou,Yitong Ding,Guanlin Zhu,Yilu Huang,Chiheng Jin,Chen Zhang,Shixuan Sun,Yu Feng,Anbang Wu,Minyi Guo,Jian Weng,Jiajin Tu,Junsong Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FIBER是一种新架构，通过将GPU SIMT模型的执行实例（fiber）从私有寄存器所有权中解耦，采用共享寄存器视图实现动态并行度扩展和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，解决现代AI工作负载中GEMM与非GEMM操作交错带来的固定并行度和粗粒度调度瓶颈。在混合精度LLM服务场景下，FIBER在Ampere上实现2.25x端到端加速，在Hopper和Blackwell上分别实现1.8x和2.09x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU越来越多地将Tensor Cores集成到执行流水线中，但调度完整的tensor计算流水线仍然困难。根本瓶颈在于两方面：(1)固定并行度——SIMT模型下并行度在执行前就被确定，无法适应非GEMM与GEMM交错变化的负载；(2)粗粒度调度——现有寄存器级调度粒度太粗，无法高效编排复杂的tensor计算流水线。因此需要一种新的架构方案来解决这两个根本性问题。

Method: FIBER提出了一个新的执行模型，其核心执行实例fiber与私有寄存器所有权解耦，仅携带最少控制状态，通过共享视图访问SM的寄存器。具体实现包括：(1)扩展ISA以支持共享寄存器寻址；(2)微架构层面实现无冲突的操作数投递；(3)编译器支持基于fiber的程序映射。这使得动态并行度扩展、细粒度寄存器级数据流调度以及无冗余的矩阵操作数供给成为可能。

Result: 在典型的混合精度LLM服务场景下，FIBER在Ampere上实现2.25x端到端加速（其中原始FP16计算部分为1.15x），在Hopper上实现1.8x加速，在Blackwell上实现2.09x加速，内核级最高可达2.49x加速。

Conclusion: FIBER通过解耦执行实例与寄存器所有权、引入共享寄存器视图的方式，有效解决了现代AI工作负载中固定并行度和粗粒度调度两大瓶颈，提供了一种冗余-free的矩阵操作数供给机制，在多种GPU架构上均取得了显著的端到端加速效果。

Abstract: Modern GPUs increasingly integrate Tensor Cores into the execution pipeline. Although aggregate tensor throughput continues to grow, aided by an operand supply that has evolved from register-based in Ampere to redundancy-free, memory-based in Hopper and Blackwell, efficiently orchestrating the complete tensor compute pipeline for the modern AI workloads remains challenging. We identify the fundamental bottlenecks as fixed parallelism and coarse-grained scheduling, both of which are exposed by modern AI workloads that interleave diverse non-GEMM operations with GEMM. To orchestrate tensor computation efficiently, we propose FIBER, a new architecture that extends the GPU SIMT (single instruction, multiple thread) model. Its basic execution instance, the \emph{fiber}, is decoupled from private register ownership, carrying only minimal control state while accessing an SM's registers through a shared view. This enables dynamic parallelism scaling, fine-grained register-level dataflow scheduling, and offers a redundancy-free alternative for matrix operand supply. We extend the ISA, microarchitecture, and compiler to realize shared-register addressing, conflict-free operand delivery, and fiber-based program mapping. Under a typical mixed-precision LLM serving scenario, FIBER achieves a 2.25x end-to-end speedup on Ampere (1.15x for the original FP16 computation), with 1.8x and 2.09x on Hopper and Blackwell respectively, and kernel-level gains up to 2.49x.

</details>


### [303] [Automated Estimation of MBIST Area and Test Time in Heterogeneous Memory IPs via Stacked Ensemble Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19705)
*Chee Jin Teoh,Ab Al-Hadi Ab Rahman,Johnny Kee Hui Wong,Premkumar A/L Kesavan Prabagaran,Muhammad Nadzir Marsono,Nuzhat Khan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于监督学习的框架，直接从RTL设计参数预测MBIST的面积和测试时间开销，无需综合。采用多项式展开、堆叠集成（XGBoost+LightGBM+NN+梯度提升元学习器）预测面积，以及XGBoost+LightGBM+Ridge回归预测测试时间，在±10%误差范围内分别达到90.68%和96.80%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式存储器在现代SoC设计中占比越来越大，MBIST虽能保证存储器可靠性但引入面积和测试时间开销。传统方法依赖完整RTL综合和测试向量生成，速度慢且资源密集，难以在早期设计规划阶段快速评估这些开销，因此需要一种无需综合的快速估算方法。

Method: 构建了包含4,470个面积样本和624个测试时间样本的数据集（通过Synopsys Design Compiler和Intel MINT工具生成）。面积预测采用多项式扩展、对数变换和缩放预处理，构建XGBoost、LightGBM和神经网络组成的堆叠集成模型，以梯度提升作为元学习器。测试时间预测采用XGBoost和LightGBM结合Ridge回归，通过100次Optuna搜索进行超参数调优。输入特征包括存储器数量、字宽、地址深度、端口配置和时钟域。

Result: 在±10%误差范围内，面积预测准确率达90.68%，测试时间预测准确率达96.80%，相较于基线方法分别提升了8.53%和48.80%。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了更快的MBIST成本估算，支持存储器IP开发中更高效的设计决策，为早期设计规划提供了无需综合的预测手段。

Abstract: Embedded memories occupy a large portion of modern System-on-Chip (SoC) designs, especially in high-performance applications such as artificial intelligence and edge computing. Memory Built-In Self-Test (MBIST) is commonly used to ensure memory reliability, but it introduces additional area and test time overhead. Accurate early estimation of these overheads is important during design planning, yet conventional methods rely on full Register Transfer Level (RTL) synthesis and test pattern generation, which are slow and resource-intensive. This study proposes a supervised learning framework that predicts MBIST area and test time directly from RTL-level design parameters without synthesis. A dataset of 4,470 samples for area and 624 for test time was generated using Synopsys Design Compiler and MINT, an Intel-enhanced MBIST tool. Input features include memory count, word width, address depth, port configuration, and clock domains. For area prediction, the features are processed through polynomial expansion, log transformation, and scaling, followed by a stacked ensemble model using XGBoost, LightGBM, and a Neural Network with Gradient Boosting as the meta-learner. For test time, XGBoost and LightGBM are combined using Ridge Regression, with hyperparameters tuned through a 100-trial Optuna search. The models achieved 90.68% accuracy for area and 96.80% for test time within a +/-10% margin, improving over baseline methods by 8.53% and 48.80% respectively.
