The results show that this approach enables faster estimation of MBIST costs and supports more efficient design decisions in memory IP development.

</details>


### [304] [Energy-Efficient Visual Inspection with FFT-Based CNNs and Adaptive Floating-Point Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19837)
*Lukas Krupp,Marco Groß,Michael Graichen,Kim Ulrich,Norbert Wehn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在工业CPU-FPGA平台上使用低精度浮点运算进行基于FFT的CNN推理。将FFT卷积与自适应训练后FP8量化相结合，提出了两种FPGA优化方法：FFT内部的渐进式偏置调整（PBA）和跨CNN的逐层指数偏置选择。实验结果表明权重缩放优于PBA，逐层偏置优化将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%，且FPGA相比纯CPU推理提升了约2.5倍的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对FFT-based CNN推理中浮点精度低的问题，探索在工业CPU-FPGA异构平台上通过减少数据路径宽度（FP8量化）来提升能效，同时研究如何通过偏置调整等优化方法减少精度降低带来的准确率损失。

Method: 将FFT卷积与自适应post-training FP8量化结合，提出两种FPGA导向优化方法：1）渐进式偏置调整（PBA），在FFT计算内部进行偏置渐进调整；2）逐层指数偏置选择，在CNN各层独立优化指数偏置。实现采用串行radix-2^2 SDF FFT模块构建LeNet-5加速器，并在工业故障检测数据集上验证。

Result: 权重缩放优于PBA方法；逐层偏置优化在不修改数据路径宽度的情况下将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%；相比纯CPU推理，FPGA实现了约2.5倍更高的能效。

Conclusion: 逐层指数偏置选择是FFT-based CNN低精度推理的有效优化手段，能在不增加硬件复杂度的前提下显著提升准确率，同时FPGA平台在能效方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: This paper investigates reduced-precision floating-point arithmetic for FFT-based CNN inference on an industrial CPU-FPGA platform. We combine FFT-based convolution with adaptive post-training FP8 quantization and evaluate two FPGA-oriented optimization methods: progressive bias adjustment (PBA) within the FFT and layer-wise exponent-bias selection across the CNN. The methods are implemented in a LeNet-5 accelerator using serial radix-$2^2$ SDF FFT modules and evaluated on an industrial fault detection dataset. Results show that weight scaling outperforms PBA, while layer-wise bias optimization increases the accuracy from 80.33% to 84.13% without modifying the datapath width. Compared with CPU-only inference, the FPGA achieves approximately 2.5$\times$ higher energy efficiency.

</details>


### [305] [Experimental Verification of Fast Voltage Droop Correction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19954)
*Shreyas Srinivas,Ian W. Jones,Carsten Schulze,Milos Krstic,Christoph Lenzen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在IHP 130nm工艺下实现的一个全数字快速自适应电压跌落响应电路，并通过测试芯片实验验证了其功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着能效优化的需要，越来越倾向于减小保护带（guard band），这导致电压跌落（voltage droop）成为限制现代VLSI电路工作频率的关键因素。动态调整时钟频率时，检测和存储电压跌落状态的器件存在亚稳态（metastability）问题。

Method: 采用IHP 130nm工艺实现了一个全数字（all-digital）的快速自适应电压跌落响应电路。电路设计细节在配套论文中描述，本文重点在于实验验证。

Result: 在测试芯片（test chip）上通过实验验证了该设计的功能正确性。

Conclusion: 该全数字自适应电压跌落响应电路在IHP 130nm工艺下实现并成功通过实验验证，能够应对因减小保护带而带来的电压跌落问题。

Abstract: Due to the trend towards minimizing guard bands for energy saving purposes, voltage droops are a key limiting factor for the operational frequency of today's VLSI circuits. Adapting clock frequencies dynamically presents the challenge of metastability in the device that detects and stores the existence of voltage droops. We present an implementation of a fast all-digital circuit for adaptive response to droops using the IHP 130 nm process. The description of the design is presented in an accompanying paper. We experimentally validate the functionality of the design on a test chip.

</details>


### [306] [ODEONN: A Digital ODE Solver Architecture for Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20110)
*Bram F. Haverkort,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ODEONN，一种模块化、可扩展的全数字振荡神经网络架构，是首个支持复值耦合的通用数字ONN，采用硬件资源减半的正弦函数近似，相比软件仿真性能退化小于2%，能量延迟积降低45倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡神经网络（ONN）是AI和组合优化问题的替代计算范式，但现有数字架构通常针对特定ONN应用设计，缺乏通用的模块化和可扩展数字架构，且现有数字ONN不支持复值耦合。

Method: 提出ODEONN架构，采用模块化可扩展设计以通用支持多种ONN应用，首次实现全数字ONN的复值耦合支持；同时提出一种正弦函数近似方法，相比标准方法节省一半硬件资源。

Result: 与全精度软件仿真相比，ODEONN的性能退化小于2%，表明定点量化和近似波形对计算精度影响较小；在传统硬件上相比软件仿真，能量延迟积降低了45倍。

Conclusion: 定点量化和近似波形仅造成很小的精度损失，ODEONN在保持高精度的同时显著提升能效，是一种可行的通用数字ONN计算架构。

Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) are an alternative computing paradigm for AI and combinatorial optimization problems. However, digital architectures are often designed for specific applications of ONNs. This work introduces a modular and scalable architecture called ODEONN that is generic to multiple applications of ONNs, and to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully digital ONN to also support complex-valued coupling. Additionally, an approximation of the sine function is introduced that uses half of the hardware resources compared to standard methods. The performance of ODEONN is compared with a full-precision software simulation, where a performance degradation of less than $2\%$ is shown. Therefore, we conclude that the fixed-point quantization and the approximated waveform affect the accuracy of computation by only a small amount. Furthermore, ODEONN shows a 45$\times$ reduction in energy-delay product over the software simulation running on conventional hardware.

</details>


### [307] [A Resource-Efficient CNN-Based EEG Auditory Attention Decoding ASIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20198)
*Qier Ma,Richard George,Stefan Scholze,Jehn Constantin,Tobias Reichenbach,Christian Mayr*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款面向实时EEG听觉注意力解码的资源高效型ASIC芯片，集成量化CNN推理引擎和Pearson相关分类器，在GF22FDX 22nm工艺下实现2.09mm²面积、0.4941mW功耗和7.34ms推理延迟，为助听应用中的鸡尾酒会问题提供高能效硬件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鸡尾酒会问题（嘈杂环境中追踪目标说话者）对人工耳蜗使用者尤其困难。虽然已有研究利用基于神经网络的EEG听觉注意力解码（AAD）来增强助听效果，但现有方案在硬件资源效率和实时性能方面仍有不足，需要更高效的计算架构来满足实际助听应用的功耗和延迟约束。

Method: 设计一种资源高效的ASIC架构，集成量化CNN推理引擎与Pearson相关分类器。硬件架构采用流式执行（streaming execution）、片上缓冲（on-chip buffering）和内存高效的数据流设计（memory-efficient dataflow），以降低硬件成本同时维持实时性能。芯片基于GF22FDX 22nm CMOS工艺实现。

Result: ASIC在GF22FDX 22nm CMOS工艺下完成全流程实现，总面积2.09mm²（1264μm×1654μm），其中CNN推理引擎和流式分类引擎仅占0.076mm²。在0.55V核心电压下功耗为0.4941mW，推理延迟仅7.34ms，实现了高能效的实时EEG听觉注意力解码。

Conclusion: 该ASIC为EEG听觉注意力解码提供了能量高效的硬件平台，适用于助听应用场景，有效解决了人工耳蜗用户在嘈杂环境中追踪目标说话者的难题。

Abstract: Following a target speaker in a noisy environment, commonly known as the cocktail party problem, remains particularly challenging for cochlear implant (CI) users. Recent studies have explored EEG-based auditory attention decoding (AAD) using neural networks to enhance hearing assistance. This paper presents a resource-efficient ASIC for real-time EEG-based auditory attention decoding by integrating a quantized CNN inference engine and a Pearson-correlation classifier. The proposed architecture employs streaming execution, on-chip buffering, and memory-efficient dataflow to reduce hardware cost while maintaining real-time performance.
  The proposed ASIC has been fully implemented in GF22FDX 22-nm CMOS technology, occupying a total silicon area of 2.09 mm$^2$(1264$μ$m x 1654$μ$m), with the CNN inference engine and streaming classification engine requiring only 0.076 mm$^2$. Operating at a core voltage of 0.55 V, the design achieves a power consumption of 0.4941 mW and an inference latency of 7.34 ms, providing an energy-efficient hardware platform for EEG-based auditory attention decoding in hearing-assistance applications.

</details>

### [308] [The Connectome and the Quest for the Functional Logic of the Drosophila Early Olfactory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19290)
*Aurel A. Lazar,Yiyin Zhou*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文回顾了果蝇早期嗅觉系统（EOS）过去十五年的连接组和突触组数据，指出前馈通路嵌入在大规模多输入多输出神经元的密集局部反馈回路中，提出理解反馈回路抽象及其在各级神经毡中输入/输出转换中的调控能力是揭示嗅觉电路功能逻辑的基础，同时强调功能逻辑的量化描述需要包含自然环境气味物对象的模型，以及必须遵循因果性的实时级联处理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管果蝇早期嗅觉系统的密集电子显微镜连接组提供了每个细胞类型、神经元和突触的高质量可视化，但接线图本身并不能揭示神经回路的功能逻辑。作者旨在超越静态连接组数据，系统性地理解反馈回路抽象及其对输入/输出转换的调控能力，并引入自然环境气味模型和因果性约束来量化嗅觉信息处理的功能逻辑。

Method: 通过对过去十五年的EOS连接组和突触组数据集进行综述分析，识别前馈通路中嵌入的密集局部反馈电路结构；提出反馈回路抽象框架以理解各级神经毡中的输入/输出转换；论证需要构建包含气味物对象的自然气味环境模型，并引入新的距离度量来分类气味语义以支持联想记忆操作；强调信息处理必须遵循因果性，将电路视为实时逐级联的巨型局部反馈环。

Result: 该综述揭示了早期嗅觉通路并非简单的单向前馈处理，而是嵌入在密集的多输入多输出局部反馈回路中；提出功能逻辑的理解需要结合反馈回路抽象、自然气味环境模型（气味语义和语法）以及因果性约束三个层面；指出需要新的距离度量来支持气味语义分类和联想记忆操作。

Conclusion: 果蝇早期嗅觉系统的功能逻辑不能仅通过静态连接组接线图来理解，系统性掌握反馈回路抽象，并结合自然环境气味模型和因果性约束，是揭示嗅觉信息处理核心原理的必经之路。

Abstract: In recent decades, the early olfactory system (EOS) of the fruit fly has become a leading model for studying olfactory processing and associative memory, owing in part to a well-characterized feedforward pathway that feeds the processes underlying associative memory and by examining the role played by a handful of neurons and synapses. The recent completion of dense electron-microscopy connectomes provides high quality visualizations of every cell type, neuron, and synapse along the early olfactory pathway. Yet a wiring diagram, however complete, does not by itself reveal the functional logic of a neural circuit. Reviewing the EOS connectome and synaptome datasets of the past fifteen years, we note that the feedforward pathway is embedded in dense local feedback circuits of large scale multi-input multi-output neurons. A systematic understanding of feedback loop abstractions, and their capacity to govern the input/output transformations at each neuropil stage, is the underlying foundation of the functional logic of the early olfactory circuits. In addition, we argue that a quantitative account of the functional logic requires an explicit model of the odorants present in the natural environment. Consisting of odorant objects, such a model defines the semantics and syntax of olfactory information processing, and calls for new distance measures for classifying the odorant semantics in support of associative memory operations. Furthermore, odor information processing must abide by causality, treating the circuit as a real-time, stage-by-stage cascade of giant local feedback loops.

</details>

### [309] [PEtab SciML: an exchange format for specifying and training dynamic scientific machine learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20184)
*Sebastian Persson,Branwen Snelling,Maren Philipps,Daniel Weindl,Marija Cvijovic,Jan Hasenauer,Dilan Pathirana,Fabian Fröhlich*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PEtab SciML——一个用于指定科学机器学习（SciML）参数估计问题的互操作数据格式，该格式支持在ODE中联合估计机理模型参数和机器学习模型参数，并提供参考Python库及Python/JAX和Julia端（AMICI和PEtab.jl）的建模支持，以及真实数据基准测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态科学机器学习（SciML）模型结合了机理ODE与ML组件，应用广泛（如学习未知生物过程、集成辅助数据模态），但目前缺乏一种可复现且高效的SciML训练标准化格式来指定参数估计问题，以支持机理和ML参数的联合估计。

Method: 提出PEtab SciML，一种互操作数据格式，用于规范定义参数估计问题，支持多种ML-ODE混合模式（如学习未知生物过程、数据模态集成）在现实问题设置中的应用，并配套参考Python库和下游建模支持（AMICI for Python/JAX，PEtab.jl for Julia），同时提供真实数据基准集合。

Result: PEtab SciML提供了跨Python和Julia生态的标准化格式及工具链支持，包含可安装的Python参考包（PyPI发布），跨平台（Linux/macOS/Windows）持续测试，并配有真实数据基准用于验证。

Conclusion: PEtab SciML为实现可复现且高效的SciML参数估计提供了标准化数据格式和生态支持，填补了机理ODE与ML联合建模中缺乏共享格式的空白，有助于推动SciML在生物过程建模等领域的应用。

Abstract: Summary: Dynamic scientific machine learning (SciML) models that combine mechanistic ordinary differential equations (ODEs) with machine learning (ML) components have applications ranging from learning unknown biological processes to integrating auxiliary data modalities into dynamic modelling. To enable reproducible and efficient SciML training, we introduce PEtab SciML, an interoperable data format for specifying parameter estimation problems in which mechanistic and ML model parameters are jointly estimated from time series data. PEtab SciML supports several ML ODE hybridization patterns in realistic problem setups. It is accompanied by a reference Python library and downstream modelling support in Python/JAX and Julia, provided by AMICI and PEtab.jl, respectively, and a collection of real data benchmarks. Availability and implementation: PEtab SciML is available on GitHub (https://github.com/PEtab-dev/petab_sciml). The reference Python package is installable from PyPI and is continuously tested and supported on Linux, macOS, and Windows.

</details>

### [310] [Hepatitis C Virus Genotyping with a Transformer Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19415)
*Ariella Aro,Taimá Furuyama,Marcelo R. S. Briones,Luis Mário R. Janini,Isabel M. V. Guedes de Carvalho,Fernando Antoneli*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于Transformer的模型在微调后对丙型肝炎病毒（HCV）基因型和亚型分类的适用性。使用来自洛斯阿拉莫斯HCV序列数据库的2,881条全基因组序列（基因型1至6），研究发现同质片段化和均衡样本分布的微调策略在基因型分类中取得了98.48%-100%的精确率，在亚型分类中达到99.89%的准确率和99.87%的精确率；而不均衡的片段化策略仅获得48.12%的精确率，且在基因型5预测上误差较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer-based模型在遗传序列分类中的适用性，特别是针对HCV基因型和亚型分类任务，验证微调策略（包括片段化方法、数据量和标注方式）对模型性能的影响。

Method: 使用2,881条HCV全基因组序列（涵盖基因型1至6及所有确认亚型，排除样本量不足的基因型7和8），基于不同片段化方法、每文件数据量和标注方式构建多个微调数据集，对Transformer模型进行微调并评估其在基因型和亚型分类中的性能。

Result: 同质片段化且样本均衡分布的微调策略在基因型分类中获得98.48%-100%的精确率；不均衡片段化和任意样本分布的策略仅获得48.12%的精确率，且基因型5预测误差高。亚型分类最佳方案达到99.89%准确率和99.87%精确率；包含额外基因型的模型因任务复杂度增加而性能略有下降。

Conclusion: 当微调数据集包含适当片段化、合理分布和正确标注的遗传序列时，Transformer-based神经网络能够实现高性能，是HCV基因型和亚型分类的有前景的方法。

Abstract: This study aims to explore the applicability of Transformer-based models for genetic sequence classification by evaluating their performance in predicting hepatitis C virus (HCV) genotypes and subtypes after fine-tuning. A total of 2,881 HCV whole-genome sequences obtained from the Los Alamos HCV Sequence Database were used, including genotypes 1 to 6 and all confirmed subtypes. Genotypes 7 and 8 were excluded due to an insufficient number of samples. The fine-tuning process was based on several datasets that differed in fragmentation method, data volume per file, and labeling. In genotype classification, fine-tuning strategies employing homogeneous fragmentation and balanced sample distribution resulted in higher performance, with precision ranging from 98.48% to 100%. In contrast, fine-tuning conducted using a fragmentation strategy that caused data imbalance, along with an arbitrary distribution of samples across training files, achieved a precision of 48.12%, which is considered low compared with other models. This configuration, which was also manually evaluated, resulted in a high error rate in genotype 5 prediction due to its low frequency in the datasets used. In subtype classification, the best-performing fine-tuning approach achieved 99.89% accuracy and 99.87% precision. Models that included additional genotypes showed a slight decrease in performance due to the increased complexity of the task. This study demonstrates that, when fine-tuning datasets contain properly fragmented, distributed, and labeled genetic sequences, Transformer-based neural networks can achieve high performance and are a promising approach for HCV genotype and subtype classification.

</details>
